
===== FILE backend/app/evaluation_components_api.py =====
"""HTTP API for operator-managed evaluation components."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import asdict
from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .evaluation_component_catalog import (
    EvaluationComponentError,
    canonical_component_hash,
    module_type_catalog,
)
from .evaluation_components import (
    component_reference_facts,
    copy_component_asset,
    create_component_asset,
    generate_component_version,
    get_component_working_copy,
    list_component_assets,
    require_component_asset,
    save_component_working_copy,
    update_component_asset,
)
from .models import (
    PromptVersion,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    User,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
)


class ComponentCreate(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    asset_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    kind: str
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    module_type_key: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=60)
    prompt_stage: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=20)
    description: str = ""


class ComponentUpdate(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    display_name: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=160)
    description: str | None = None
    is_discoverable: bool | None = None


class ComponentCopy(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    target_category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    target_asset_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)


class WorkingCopySave(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    expected_lock_version: int = Field(ge=1)
    content: dict[str, Any]


def _http_error(exc: EvaluationComponentError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=exc.status_code,
        detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)},
    )


def _require_permission(user: User, permission: str) -> None:
    if has_permission(user, permission):
        return
    code = (
        "component_version_forbidden"
        if permission == "evaluation_components:version"
        else "component_write_forbidden"
    )
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=403,
        detail={"code": code, "message": "当前账号没有所需的组件管理权限"},
    )


def _asset_payload(row: WorkflowNodeAsset) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "asset_key": row.asset_key,
        "kind": row.kind,
        "display_name": row.display_name,
        "module_type_key": row.module_type_key,
        "description": row.description,
        "is_discoverable": row.is_discoverable,
        "copied_from_asset_id": row.copied_from_asset_id,
        "created_by": row.created_by,
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "updated_at": row.updated_at,
    }


def _working_copy_payload(row) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "asset_id": row.asset_id,
        "content": json.loads(row.content_json),
        "editor_schema_version": row.editor_schema_version,
        "lock_version": row.lock_version,
        "updated_by": row.updated_by,
        "updated_at": row.updated_at,
    }


def _version_items(db: Session, asset: WorkflowNodeAsset) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if asset.kind == "rule_module":
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(RuleModuleVersion)
            .where(RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id)
            .order_by(RuleModuleVersion.version.desc())
        ).all()
        return [
            {
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "id": row.id,
                "version": str(row.version),
                "content_hash": row.content_hash,
                "content": json.loads(row.content_json),
                "source_working_copy_revision": row.source_working_copy_revision,
                "created_by": row.created_by,
                "created_at": row.created_at,
                "reference_facts": component_reference_facts(
                    db, rule_module_version_id=row.id
                ),
            }
            for row in rows
        ]
    prompt_ids = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id).where(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id.is_not(None),
        )
    ).all()
    rows = (
        db.scalars(
            select(PromptVersion)
            .where(PromptVersion.id.in_(prompt_ids))
            .order_by(PromptVersion.id.desc())
        ).all()
        if prompt_ids
        else []
    )
    return [
        {
            "kind": "prompt",
            "id": row.id,
            "version": row.version,
            "content": {
                "stage": row.stage,
                "system_prompt": row.system_prompt,
                "user_prompt": row.user_prompt,
                "rubric_version": row.rubric_version,
            },
            "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(
                {
                    "stage": row.stage,
                    "system_prompt": row.system_prompt,
                    "user_prompt": row.user_prompt,
                    "rubric_version": row.rubric_version,
                }
            ),
            "created_by": row.created_by,
            "created_at": row.created_at,
            "reference_facts": component_reference_facts(
                db, prompt_version_id=row.id
            ),
        }
        for row in rows
    ]


def build_evaluation_components_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/evaluation-components", tags=["evaluation-components"]
    )

    @router.get("/module-types")
    def list_module_types(
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"items": [asdict(item) for item in module_type_catalog()]}

    @router.get("")
    def list_assets(
        category_key: str = Query(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        include_hidden: bool = False,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "items": [
                _asset_payload(row)
                for row in list_component_assets(
                    db,
                    category_key=category_key,
                    include_hidden=include_hidden,
                )
            ]
        }

    @router.post("", status_code=201)
    def create_asset(
        payload: ComponentCreate,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:write")
        try:
            row = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key=payload.category_key,
                asset_key=payload.asset_key,
                kind=payload.kind,
                display_name=payload.display_name,
                module_type_key=payload.module_type_key,
                prompt_stage=payload.prompt_stage,
                description=payload.description,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _asset_payload(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{asset_id}")
    def get_asset(
        asset_id: int,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return _asset_payload(require_component_asset(db, asset_id))
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.patch("/{asset_id}")
    def update_asset(
        asset_id: int,
        payload: ComponentUpdate,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:write")
        try:
            row = update_component_asset(
                db,
                asset_id=asset_id,
                display_name=payload.display_name,
                description=payload.description,
                is_discoverable=payload.is_discoverable,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _asset_payload(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{asset_id}/copy", status_code=201)
    def copy_asset(
        asset_id: int,
        payload: ComponentCopy,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:write")
        try:
            row = copy_component_asset(
                db,
                source_asset_id=asset_id,
                target_category_key=payload.target_category_key,
                target_asset_key=payload.target_asset_key,
                display_name=payload.display_name,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _asset_payload(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{asset_id}/working-copy")
    def get_working_copy(
        asset_id: int,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return _working_copy_payload(
                get_component_working_copy(db, asset_id)
            )
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.put("/{asset_id}/working-copy")
    def save_working_copy(
        asset_id: int,
        payload: WorkingCopySave,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:write")
        try:
            row = save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=asset_id,
                expected_lock_version=payload.expected_lock_version,
                content=payload.content,
                updated_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _working_copy_payload(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{asset_id}/versions", status_code=201)
    def generate_version(
        asset_id: int,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:version")
        try:
            row = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset_id, created_by=user.username
            )
            db.commit()
            return asdict(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{asset_id}/versions")
    def list_versions(
        asset_id: int,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return {
                "items": _version_items(
                    db, require_component_asset(db, asset_id)
                )
            }
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{asset_id}/versions/{version_id}/references")
    def version_references(
        asset_id: int,
        version_id: int,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, bool]:
        try:
            asset = require_component_asset(db, asset_id)
            if asset.kind == "prompt":
                binding = db.scalar(
                    select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id).where(
                        WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
                        WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == version_id,
                    )
                )
                if binding is None:
                    raise EvaluationComponentError(
                        "component_version_unknown",
                        "组件版本不存在",
                        status_code=404,
                    )
                return component_reference_facts(
                    db, prompt_version_id=version_id
                )
            row = db.scalar(
                select(RuleModuleVersion.id).where(
                    RuleModuleVersion.id == version_id,
                    RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id,
                )
            )
            if row is None:
                raise EvaluationComponentError(
                    "component_version_unknown",
                    "组件版本不存在",
                    status_code=404,
                )
            return component_reference_facts(
                db, rule_module_version_id=version_id
            )
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/evaluation_credentials.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import (
    EvaluationJob,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelNodeBinding,
    ModelQuotaLease,
    ModelQuotaPool,
)
from .security import secret_reference_platform_compatible


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelConcurrencyResource:
    """One real provider-capacity scope used by an incremental job.

    Legacy models deliberately share one compatibility resource so multiple
    unbound credentials cannot accidentally multiply the old global limit.
    """

    key: str
    limit: int
    quota_pool_id: int | None
    dispatchable: bool


def model_has_credentials(model: ModelConfig | None) -> bool:
    return bool(
        model is not None
        and model.encrypted_api_key
        and model.encrypted_api_key.strip()
        and secret_reference_platform_compatible(model.encrypted_api_key)
    )


def default_evaluation_model(db: Session) -> ModelConfig | None:
    binding = db.scalar(
        select(ModelNodeBinding).where(
            ModelNodeBinding.node_key == "evaluation_main",
            ModelNodeBinding.category_key.is_(None),
            ModelNodeBinding.enabled.is_(True),
        )
    )
    if (
        binding is not None
        and binding.model.active
        and model_has_credentials(binding.model)
    ):
        return binding.model
    for model in db.scalars(
        select(ModelConfig)
        .where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True))
        .order_by(ModelConfig.id.asc())
    ):
        if model_has_credentials(model):
            return model
    return None


def frozen_job_model_ids(job: EvaluationJob) -> tuple[int, ...] | None:
    if not job.category_profile_snapshot_json:
        return None
    try:
        snapshot = json.loads(job.category_profile_snapshot_json)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return ()
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        return ()
    main_id = snapshot.get("model_config_id")
    if not isinstance(main_id, int) or isinstance(main_id, bool) or main_id < 1:
        return ()
    model_ids = [main_id]
    summary_id = snapshot.get("pdf_summary_model_config_id")
    if summary_id is not None:
        if (
            not isinstance(summary_id, int)
            or isinstance(summary_id, bool)
            or summary_id < 1
        ):
            return ()
        model_ids.append(summary_id)
    return tuple(dict.fromkeys(model_ids))


def job_primary_model(db: Session, job: EvaluationJob) -> ModelConfig | None:
    model_ids = frozen_job_model_ids(job)
    if not model_ids:
        return None
    return db.get(ModelConfig, model_ids[0])


def job_models(
    db: Session,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    *,
    fallback_model: ModelConfig | None,
) -> tuple[ModelConfig, ...]:
    model_ids = frozen_job_model_ids(job)
    if model_ids is None:
        return (fallback_model,) if fallback_model is not None else ()
    if not model_ids:
        return ()
    models = tuple(db.get(ModelConfig, model_id) for model_id in model_ids)
    if any(model is None for model in models):
        return ()
    return tuple(model for model in models if model is not None)


def _as_utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def job_concurrency_resources(
    db: Session,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    *,
    fallback_model: ModelConfig | None,
    now: datetime | None = None,
    active_quota_leases: Mapping[int, int] | None = None,
) -> tuple[ModelConcurrencyResource, ...]:
    """Resolve distinct quota scopes without exposing credential material."""

    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if current.tzinfo is None:
        current = current.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    resources: dict[str, ModelConcurrencyResource] = {}
    for model in job_models(db, job, fallback_model=fallback_model):
        if not model_has_credentials(model):
            return ()
        if model.quota_pool_id is None:
            legacy_limit = max(
                1,
                int(
                    getattr(fallback_model, "max_concurrency", 0)
                    or model.max_concurrency
                    or 1
                ),
            )
            resources["legacy-global"] = ModelConcurrencyResource(
                key="legacy-global",
                limit=legacy_limit,
                quota_pool_id=None,
                dispatchable=True,
            )
            continue
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, model.quota_pool_id)
        if pool is None:
            return ()
        cooling_down = (
            pool.cooldown_until is not None
            and _as_utc(pool.cooldown_until) > _as_utc(current)
        )
        active_leases = (
            active_quota_leases.get(pool.id, 0)
            if active_quota_leases is not None
            else db.scalar(
                select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.pool_id == pool.id,
                    ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
                    ModelQuotaLease.expires_at > current,
                )
            )
            or 0
        )
        resources[f"quota-pool:{pool.id}"] = ModelConcurrencyResource(
            key=f"quota-pool:{pool.id}",
            limit=max(1, pool.max_concurrency),
            quota_pool_id=pool.id,
            dispatchable=bool(
                pool.enabled
                and not cooling_down
                and active_leases < pool.max_concurrency
            ),
        )
    return tuple(resources[key] for key in sorted(resources))


def incremental_global_concurrency_limit(
    db: Session,
    *,
    fallback_model: ModelConfig,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> int:
    """Sum genuinely independent provider pools once each.

    Unbound models retain one legacy global bucket. This is intentionally not
    a sum of API keys or model rows.
    """

    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if current.tzinfo is None:
        current = current.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    limits: dict[str, int] = {}
    has_legacy = False
    models = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(ModelConfig)
            .where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True))
            .order_by(ModelConfig.id.asc())
        )
    )
    if all(model.id != fallback_model.id for model in models):
        models.append(fallback_model)
    for model in models:
        if not model_has_credentials(model):
            continue
        if model.quota_pool_id is None:
            has_legacy = True
            continue
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, model.quota_pool_id)
        if pool is None or not pool.enabled:
            continue
        if (
            pool.cooldown_until is not None
            and _as_utc(pool.cooldown_until) > _as_utc(current)
        ):
            continue
        limits[f"quota-pool:{pool.id}"] = max(1, pool.max_concurrency)
    if has_legacy:
        limits["legacy-global"] = max(1, fallback_model.max_concurrency)
    return max(1, sum(limits.values()))


def job_has_required_credentials(
    db: Session,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    *,
    fallback_model: ModelConfig | None,
) -> bool:
    model_ids = frozen_job_model_ids(job)
    if model_ids is None:
        return model_has_credentials(fallback_model)
    if not model_ids:
        return False
    return all(model_has_credentials(db.get(ModelConfig, model_id)) for model_id in model_ids)

===== FILE backend/app/evaluation_mechanism_migration.py =====
"""Deterministic, auditable slicing of legacy evaluation revisions.

This module creates component assets and immutable rule-module histories. A
new, untouched operator working copy is initialized from the latest imported
revision so editing starts from the real current contract. It deliberately
does not create workflow versions or change incremental bindings; those
higher-risk operations belong to the workflow-v3 migration.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
import json
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .category_evaluation_v3_revisions import revision_bundle
from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json
from .evaluation_component_catalog import (
    MODULE_TYPE_KEYS,
    canonical_component_hash,
    module_type_catalog,
    validate_module_content,
)
from .evaluation_components import create_component_asset
from .models import (
    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_assets import bind_rule_module_version
from .level_scale import CANONICAL_LEVELS, resolve_level_scale

    "contract.common_modifiers.media_type_penalty": "media_adjustment",

WORKFLOW_LEVEL_FIELDS = frozenset(
    {
        "category_key",
        "revision",
        "contract_hash",
        "display_name",
        "contract.schema_version",
        "contract.spec_version",
        "contract.category_key",
        "contract.profile_type",
        "contract.level_semantics_version",
        "contract.prompt_bindings",
        "contract.common_modifiers.format_version",
        "contract.aesthetic_foundation.format_version",
        "contract.aesthetic_foundation.call_b_version",
        "contract.aesthetic_foundation.calibration_status",
        # Historical compatibility switches remain workflow metadata instead
        # of polluting the strictly isolated anchor-image component.
        "contract.aesthetic_foundation.anchor_comparison_enabled",
        "contract.aesthetic_foundation.use_anchors",
        # image_defects 前检开关是工作流级合同元数据（关闭时水印并入红线），
        # 横跨调用A的 schema/正文/审计三处，不属于任何单独的规则模块。
        "contract.image_defects_enabled",
        # Human-evidence precedence governs correction/recommendation flow,
        # not one independently executable scoring module.
        "contract.evidence_governance",
        # 3D&SU freezes its model-output contract and B-score foundation at
        # workflow level.  They describe prompt/result I/O rather than one of
        # the seven independently versioned rule modules.
        "contract.output_contract",
        "contract.b_aesthetic_foundation",
    }
)

_DEFAULT_LEVEL_SCALE = {
    "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
    "levels": [
        {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
        {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
        {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40},
        {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 20},
        {"level": "L5", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
    ],
}


class EvaluationMechanismMigrationError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MigrationCompatibilityReport:
    compatible: bool
    unclassified_paths: tuple[str, ...]
    multiply_owned_paths: tuple[str, ...]
    different_paths: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CategoryComponentMigrationResult:
    category_key: str
    created_counts: dict[str, int]
    revision_slices: dict[int, dict[str, int]]
    compatibility_reports: dict[int, MigrationCompatibilityReport]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CategoryRevisionHistoryMigrationResult:
    category_key: str
    created_counts: dict[str, int]
    revision_slices: dict[int, dict[str, int]]
    compatibility_reports: dict[int, MigrationCompatibilityReport]
    mapping_ids: dict[int, int]


class LegacyModuleSlices(dict[str, dict[str, Any]]):
    """Seven module contents plus non-duplicated projection metadata."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        values: Mapping[str, dict[str, Any]],
        *,
        workflow_fields: Mapping[str, Any],
        unclassified_paths: Iterable[str],
        multiply_owned_paths: Iterable[str],
        presence: Iterable[str],
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(values)
        self.workflow_fields = deepcopy(dict(workflow_fields))
        self.unclassified_paths = tuple(sorted(set(unclassified_paths)))
        self.multiply_owned_paths = tuple(sorted(set(multiply_owned_paths)))
        self.presence = frozenset(presence)


def _get_path(root: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
    current: Any = root
    for part in path.split("."):
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or part not in current:
            return False, None
        current = current[part]
    return True, current


def _set_path(root: dict[str, Any], path: str, value: Any) -> None:
    parts = path.split(".")
    current = root
    for part in parts[:-1]:
        next_value = current.get(part)
        if not isinstance(next_value, dict):
            next_value = {}
            current[part] = next_value
        current = next_value
    current[parts[-1]] = deepcopy(value)


def _leaf_paths(value: Any, prefix: str = "") -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        if not value:
            return [prefix] if prefix else []
        paths: list[str] = []
        for key, child in value.items():
            path = f"{prefix}.{key}" if prefix else str(key)
            paths.extend(_leaf_paths(child, path))
        return paths
    if isinstance(value, list):
        if not value:
            return [prefix] if prefix else []
        paths = []
        for index, child in enumerate(value):
            paths.extend(_leaf_paths(child, f"{prefix}.{index}"))
        return paths
    return [prefix] if prefix else []


def _path_matches(path: str, prefix: str) -> bool:
    return path == prefix or path.startswith(f"{prefix}.")


def _ownership_audit(bundle: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
    unclassified: list[str] = []
    multiply_owned: list[str] = []
    for path in _leaf_paths(bundle):
        owners = {
            owner
            for prefix, owner in LEGACY_FIELD_OWNERS.items()
            if _path_matches(path, prefix)
        }
        workflow_owned = any(
            _path_matches(path, prefix) for prefix in WORKFLOW_LEVEL_FIELDS
        )
        if len(owners) > 1:
            multiply_owned.append(path)
        elif not owners and not workflow_owned:
            unclassified.append(path)
    return unclassified, multiply_owned


def _workflow_fields(bundle: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    projected: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for path in WORKFLOW_LEVEL_FIELDS:
        exists, value = _get_path(bundle, path)
        if exists:
            _set_path(projected, path, value)
    return projected


def _copy_or_default(
    bundle: Mapping[str, Any], path: str, default: Any
) -> tuple[Any, bool]:
    exists, value = _get_path(bundle, path)
    return (deepcopy(value), True) if exists else (deepcopy(default), False)


def split_legacy_revision(bundle: Mapping[str, Any]) -> LegacyModuleSlices:
    if not isinstance(bundle, Mapping):
        raise EvaluationMechanismMigrationError(
            "legacy_bundle_invalid", "旧评测机制必须是对象", status_code=400
        )
    unclassified, multiply_owned = _ownership_audit(bundle)
    presence: set[str] = set()

    redline, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle,
        "contract.redline_policy",
        {
            "format_version": "redline-policy-v1",
            "enabled": False,
            "hit_level": "L5",
            "hit_score_cap": 49,
            "rules": [],
        },
    )
    if present:
        presence.add("contract.redline_policy")
    precheck, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "contract.authoritative_precheck_contract", None
    )
    if present:
        redline["authoritative_precheck_contract"] = precheck
        presence.add("contract.authoritative_precheck_contract")

    track, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle,
        "contract.track_classification",
        {
            "format_version": "track-classification-v1",
            "tracks": [
                {
                    "key": "legacy_default",
                    "label": "历史默认赛道",
                    "base_score": 0,
                    "dimension_max": 100,
                    "track_cap": 100,
                    "dimension_schema_ref": {
                        "schema_key": "legacy_default",
                        "version": "1.0.0",
                    },
                }
            ],
            "default_track": "legacy_default",
        },
    )
    if present:
        presence.add("contract.track_classification")
    classification_map, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "classification_map", {}
    )
    track["classification_map"] = classification_map
    if present:
        presence.add("classification_map")
    adjustments, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "contract.track_adjustments", None
    )
    if present:
        track["track_adjustments"] = adjustments
        presence.add("contract.track_adjustments")

    media, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle,
        "contract.common_modifiers.media_type_penalty",
        {"enabled": False, "penalties": {}},
    )
    if present:
        presence.add("contract.common_modifiers.media_type_penalty")
    media_enabled, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "media_penalty_enabled", False
    )
    media["media_penalty_enabled"] = media_enabled
    if present:
        presence.add("media_penalty_enabled")

    quality: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for source_path, content_key in (
        ("contract.common_modifiers.high_score_veto", "high_score_veto"),
        (
            "contract.common_modifiers.hard_defect_penalty",
            "hard_defect_penalty",
        ),
        ("contract.quality_rules", "quality_rules"),
        ("contract.hard_defect_penalty", "legacy_hard_defect_penalty"),
        (
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.boundary_policy",
            "legacy_boundary_policy",
        ),
        (
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
            "legacy_casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
        ),
        (
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.hard_defect_exemptions",
            "legacy_hard_defect_exemptions",
        ),
    ):
        value, present = _copy_or_default(bundle, source_path, None)
        if present:
            quality[content_key] = value
            presence.add(source_path)

    dimensions, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "subcategory_dimensions", {}
    )
    if present:
        presence.add("subcategory_dimensions")
    deduction_rules, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "dimension_deduction_rules", {}
    )
    if present:
        presence.add("dimension_deduction_rules")

    legacy_dimension_keys, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "contract.aesthetic_foundation.dimension_keys", None
    )
    if present:
        presence.add("contract.aesthetic_foundation.dimension_keys")
    dimension_applicability_policy, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle,
        "contract.aesthetic_foundation.dimension_applicability_policy",
        None,
    )
    if present:
        presence.add(
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.dimension_applicability_policy"
        )
    holistic_aesthetic, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "contract.aesthetic_foundation.holistic_aesthetic", None
    )
    if present:
        presence.add("contract.aesthetic_foundation.holistic_aesthetic")

    anchor, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle,
        "contract.anchor_mechanism",
        {"enabled": False, "max_anchor_images": 5, "anchors": []},
    )
    if present:
        presence.add("contract.anchor_mechanism")
    legacy_anchors, has_legacy_anchors = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "contract.aesthetic_foundation.anchors", None
    )
    if has_legacy_anchors:
        presence.add("contract.aesthetic_foundation.anchors")
        if "contract.anchor_mechanism" not in presence:
            normalized_anchors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
            for item in legacy_anchors if isinstance(legacy_anchors, list) else []:
                if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
                    continue
                normalized = {
                    key: deepcopy(item[key])
                    for key in ("level", "asset_id", "mime_type", "sha256")
                    if key in item
                }
                stored_name = item.get("stored_name")
                if isinstance(stored_name, str) and stored_name:
                    normalized["note"] = stored_name
                normalized_anchors.append(normalized)
            anchor = {
                "spec_version": "anchor-mechanism-v1",
                "enabled": bool(normalized_anchors),
                "max_anchor_images": max(5, len(normalized_anchors)),
                "anchors": normalized_anchors,
            }

    level_scale, has_level_scale = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "contract.level_scale", None
    )
    if has_level_scale:
        presence.add("contract.level_scale")
    else:
        thresholds, has_thresholds = _copy_or_default(
            bundle, "contract.level_thresholds", None
        )
        if has_thresholds:
            resolved = resolve_level_scale(
                {"level_thresholds": deepcopy(thresholds)}
            )
            enabled_thresholds = {
                entry["level"]: entry["min_score"]
                for entry in resolved["thresholds"]
            }
            level_scale = {
                "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
                "levels": [
                    (
                        {
                            "level": level,
                            "enabled": True,
                            "min_score": enabled_thresholds[level],
                        }
                        if level in enabled_thresholds
                        else {"level": level, "enabled": False}
                    )
                    for level in CANONICAL_LEVELS
                ],
                "legacy_level_thresholds": thresholds,
            }
            presence.add("contract.level_thresholds")
        else:
            level_scale = deepcopy(_DEFAULT_LEVEL_SCALE)
    legacy_score_thresholds, present = _copy_or_default(
        bundle, "contract.aesthetic_foundation.score_thresholds", None
    )
    if present:
        level_scale["legacy_score_thresholds"] = legacy_score_thresholds
        presence.add("contract.aesthetic_foundation.score_thresholds")

    values = {
        "eligibility_redline": redline,
        "track_routing": track,
        "media_adjustment": media,
        "quality_cap_exception": quality,
        "dimension_scoring": {
            "subcategory_dimensions": dimensions,
            "dimension_deduction_rules": deduction_rules,
            **(
                {"legacy_dimension_keys": legacy_dimension_keys}
                if legacy_dimension_keys is not None
                else {}
            ),
            **(
                {
                    "dimension_applicability_policy": (
                        dimension_applicability_policy
                    )
                }
                if dimension_applicability_policy is not None
                else {}
            ),
            **(
                {"holistic_aesthetic": holistic_aesthetic}
                if holistic_aesthetic is not None
                else {}
            ),
        },
        "anchor_calibration": anchor,
        "score_level_matcher": level_scale,
    }
    return LegacyModuleSlices(
        values,
        workflow_fields=_workflow_fields(bundle),
        unclassified_paths=unclassified,
        multiply_owned_paths=multiply_owned,
        presence=presence,
    )


def _content_without(
    content: Mapping[str, Any], *keys: str
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        key: deepcopy(value)
        for key, value in content.items()
        if key not in set(keys)
    }


def project_modules_to_legacy_bundle(
    slices: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    missing = sorted(set(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS) - set(slices))
    if missing:
        raise EvaluationMechanismMigrationError(
            "legacy_module_slice_incomplete",
            f"模块切片不完整，缺少：{','.join(missing)}",
            status_code=400,
        )
    projected = deepcopy(
        getattr(slices, "workflow_fields", {})
    )
    presence = getattr(slices, "presence", frozenset(LEGACY_FIELD_OWNERS))

    redline = dict(slices["eligibility_redline"])
    if "contract.redline_policy" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.redline_policy",
            _content_without(redline, "authoritative_precheck_contract"),
        )
    if "contract.authoritative_precheck_contract" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.authoritative_precheck_contract",
            redline.get("authoritative_precheck_contract"),
        )

    track = dict(slices["track_routing"])
    if "contract.track_classification" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.track_classification",
            _content_without(track, "classification_map", "track_adjustments"),
        )
    if "classification_map" in presence:
        _set_path(projected, "classification_map", track.get("classification_map", {}))
    if "contract.track_adjustments" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected, "contract.track_adjustments", track.get("track_adjustments")
        )

    media = dict(slices["media_adjustment"])
    if "contract.common_modifiers.media_type_penalty" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.common_modifiers.media_type_penalty",
            _content_without(media, "media_penalty_enabled"),
        )
    if "media_penalty_enabled" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "media_penalty_enabled",
            media.get("media_penalty_enabled", False),
        )

    quality = slices["quality_cap_exception"]
    for source_path, content_key in (
        ("contract.common_modifiers.high_score_veto", "high_score_veto"),
        (
            "contract.common_modifiers.hard_defect_penalty",
            "hard_defect_penalty",
        ),
        ("contract.quality_rules", "quality_rules"),
        ("contract.hard_defect_penalty", "legacy_hard_defect_penalty"),
        (
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.boundary_policy",
            "legacy_boundary_policy",
        ),
        (
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
            "legacy_casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
        ),
        (
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.hard_defect_exemptions",
            "legacy_hard_defect_exemptions",
        ),
    ):
        if source_path in presence:
            _set_path(projected, source_path, quality.get(content_key))

    dimensions = slices["dimension_scoring"]
    if "subcategory_dimensions" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "subcategory_dimensions",
            dimensions.get("subcategory_dimensions", {}),
        )
    if "dimension_deduction_rules" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "dimension_deduction_rules",
            dimensions.get("dimension_deduction_rules", {}),
        )
    if "contract.aesthetic_foundation.dimension_keys" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.dimension_keys",
            dimensions.get("legacy_dimension_keys"),
        )
    if (
        "contract.aesthetic_foundation.dimension_applicability_policy"
        in presence
    ):
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.dimension_applicability_policy",
            dimensions.get("dimension_applicability_policy"),
        )
    if "contract.aesthetic_foundation.holistic_aesthetic" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.holistic_aesthetic",
            dimensions.get("holistic_aesthetic"),
        )
    if "contract.anchor_mechanism" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.anchor_mechanism",
            slices["anchor_calibration"],
        )
    if "contract.aesthetic_foundation.anchors" in presence:
        legacy_anchors = []
        for item in slices["anchor_calibration"].get("anchors", []):
            if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
                continue
            legacy_item = {
                key: deepcopy(item[key])
                for key in ("asset_id", "level", "mime_type", "sha256")
                if key in item
            }
            note = item.get("note")
            if isinstance(note, str) and note:
                legacy_item["stored_name"] = note
            legacy_anchors.append(legacy_item)
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.anchors",
            legacy_anchors,
        )
    if "contract.level_scale" in presence:
        level_scale = dict(slices["score_level_matcher"])
        if "contract.level_thresholds" in presence:
            level_scale.pop("legacy_level_thresholds", None)
        if "contract.aesthetic_foundation.score_thresholds" in presence:
            level_scale.pop("legacy_score_thresholds", None)
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.level_scale",
            level_scale,
        )
    if "contract.level_thresholds" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.level_thresholds",
            slices["score_level_matcher"].get("legacy_level_thresholds"),
        )
    if "contract.aesthetic_foundation.score_thresholds" in presence:
        _set_path(
            projected,
            "contract.aesthetic_foundation.score_thresholds",
            slices["score_level_matcher"].get("legacy_score_thresholds"),
        )
    return projected


def _different_paths(left: Any, right: Any) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    different: set[str] = set()

    def visit(left_value: Any, right_value: Any, path: str) -> None:
        if isinstance(left_value, Mapping) and isinstance(right_value, Mapping):
            for key in set(left_value) | set(right_value):
                child_path = f"{path}.{key}" if path else str(key)
                if key not in left_value:
                    different.update(_leaf_paths(right_value[key], child_path))
                elif key not in right_value:
                    different.update(_leaf_paths(left_value[key], child_path))
                else:
                    visit(left_value[key], right_value[key], child_path)
            return
        if isinstance(left_value, list) and isinstance(right_value, list):
            maximum = max(len(left_value), len(right_value))
            for index in range(maximum):
                child_path = f"{path}.{index}" if path else str(index)
                if index >= len(left_value):
                    different.update(_leaf_paths(right_value[index], child_path))
                elif index >= len(right_value):
                    different.update(_leaf_paths(left_value[index], child_path))
                else:
                    visit(left_value[index], right_value[index], child_path)
            return
        if left_value != right_value:
            different.add(path)

    visit(left, right, "")
    return tuple(sorted(different))


def compare_legacy_projection(
    legacy_bundle: Mapping[str, Any], projected_bundle: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> MigrationCompatibilityReport:
    slices = split_legacy_revision(legacy_bundle)
    different = _different_paths(legacy_bundle, projected_bundle)
    return MigrationCompatibilityReport(
        compatible=not (
            slices.unclassified_paths
            or slices.multiply_owned_paths
            or different
        ),
        unclassified_paths=slices.unclassified_paths,
        multiply_owned_paths=slices.multiply_owned_paths,
        different_paths=different,
    )


def _require_module_asset(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    module_type_key: str,
    actor: str,
) -> tuple[WorkflowNodeAsset, bool]:
    asset = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == module_type_key,
        )
    )
    if asset is not None:
        if (
            asset.kind != "rule_module"
            or asset.module_type_key != module_type_key
        ):
            raise EvaluationMechanismMigrationError(
                "legacy_module_asset_conflict",
                f"已有节点 {module_type_key} 的类型与迁移目标不一致",
            )
        return asset, False
    definitions = {item.key: item for item in module_type_catalog()}
    definition = definitions[module_type_key]
    return (
        create_component_asset(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            asset_key=module_type_key,
            kind="rule_module",
            display_name=definition.display_name,
            module_type_key=module_type_key,
            description=definition.description,
            created_by=actor,
        ),
        True,
    )


def _next_module_version(db: Session, asset_id: int) -> int:
    maximum = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(RuleModuleVersion.version)).where(
            RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset_id
        )
    )
    return int(maximum or 0) + 1


def _require_module_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    asset: WorkflowNodeAsset,
    module_type_key: str,
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    actor: str,
) -> tuple[RuleModuleVersion, bool]:
    normalized = validate_module_content(module_type_key, content)
    digest = canonical_component_hash(normalized)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(RuleModuleVersion).where(
            RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id,
            RuleModuleVersion.content_hash == digest,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing, False
    row = RuleModuleVersion(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        version=_next_module_version(db, asset.id),
        module_type_key=module_type_key,
        content_json=canonical_json(normalized),
        content_hash=digest,
        source_working_copy_revision=1,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    bind_rule_module_version(
        db, asset=asset, rule_module_version=row, created_by=actor
    )
    return row, True


def _bootstrap_module_working_copy(
    db: Session,
    *,
    asset: WorkflowNodeAsset,
    latest_version: RuleModuleVersion,
    actor: str,
) -> None:
    working = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy).where(
            EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == asset.id
        )
    )
    if working is None:
        return
    try:
        current_content = json.loads(working.content_json)
    except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return
    if (
        current_content != {}
        or working.base_rule_module_version_id is not None
        or working.lock_version != 1
    ):
        return
    working.content_json = latest_version.content_json
    working.base_rule_module_version_id = latest_version.id
    working.updated_by = actor


def migrate_category_component_history(
    db: Session, category_key: str, *, actor: str
) -> CategoryComponentMigrationResult:
    revisions = db.scalars(
        select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision)
        .where(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == category_key)
        .order_by(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.revision.asc(),
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.id.asc(),
        )
    ).all()
    created_assets = 0
    created_versions = 0
    revision_slices: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    reports: dict[int, MigrationCompatibilityReport] = {}
    assets: dict[str, WorkflowNodeAsset] = {}
    latest_versions: dict[str, RuleModuleVersion] = {}

    for module_type_key in MODULE_TYPE_KEYS:
        asset, created = _require_module_asset(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            module_type_key=module_type_key,
            actor=actor,
        )
        assets[module_type_key] = asset
        created_assets += int(created)

    for revision in revisions:
        legacy = revision_bundle(revision)
        slices = split_legacy_revision(legacy)
        projected = project_modules_to_legacy_bundle(slices)
        reports[revision.id] = compare_legacy_projection(legacy, projected)
        revision_slices[revision.id] = {}
        for module_type_key in MODULE_TYPE_KEYS:
            version, created = _require_module_version(
                db,
                asset=assets[module_type_key],
                module_type_key=module_type_key,
                content=slices[module_type_key],
                actor=actor,
            )
            revision_slices[revision.id][module_type_key] = version.id
            latest_versions[module_type_key] = version
            created_versions += int(created)

    for module_type_key, latest_version in latest_versions.items():
        _bootstrap_module_working_copy(
            db,
            asset=assets[module_type_key],
            latest_version=latest_version,
            actor=actor,
        )
    db.flush()

    return CategoryComponentMigrationResult(
        category_key=category_key,
        created_counts={
            "assets": created_assets,
            "component_versions": created_versions,
        },
        revision_slices=revision_slices,
        compatibility_reports=reports,
    )


def _history_node(
    node_id: str,
    kind: str,
    display_name: str,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": display_name,
        "config": dict(config),
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 1,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": ["request_id", "trace_id", "legacy_revision_id"],
    }


def _legacy_revision_manifest(
    *,
    category_key: str,
    revision_id: int,
    module_versions: Mapping[str, int],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    nodes = [
        _history_node(
            "upstream",
            "upstream_connector",
            "读取历史评测输入",
            {"connector_key": f"logical.{category_key}.legacy_input"},
        )
    ]
    previous = "upstream"
    edges: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    definitions = {item.key: item for item in module_type_catalog()}
    for index, module_type_key in enumerate(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS, start=1):
        node_id = f"{module_type_key}_{index}"
        nodes.append(
            _history_node(
                node_id,
                "rule_module",
                definitions[module_type_key].display_name,
                {
                    "asset_key": module_type_key,
                    "module_type_key": module_type_key,
                    "rule_module_version_id": module_versions[module_type_key],
                    "input_bindings": {
                        "legacy_input": f"{previous}.output"
                    },
                    "scope": f"legacy_revision_{revision_id}",
                },
            )
        )
        edges.append({"from": previous, "to": node_id})
        previous = node_id
    nodes.append(
        _history_node(
            "downstream",
            "downstream_projection",
            "输出历史评测结果",
            {"connector_key": f"logical.{category_key}.legacy_output"},
        )
    )
    edges.append({"from": previous, "to": "downstream"})
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "lane_key": "legacy_evaluation",
        "run_modes": ["evaluation", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": edges,
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
    }


def _history_definition(
    db: Session, category_key: str, *, actor: str
) -> tuple[WorkflowDefinition, bool]:
    workflow_key = f"{category_key}.evaluation"
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
            WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == workflow_key
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        try:
            allowed = json.loads(existing.allowed_categories_json)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise EvaluationMechanismMigrationError(
                "legacy_workflow_definition_invalid",
                "旧评测机制迁移工作流定义损坏",
            ) from exc
        if allowed != [category_key]:
            raise EvaluationMechanismMigrationError(
                "legacy_workflow_definition_conflict",
                "已有迁移工作流定义的类目范围不一致",
            )
        return existing, False
    from .workflow_registry import create_workflow_definition

    return (
        create_workflow_definition(
            db,
            workflow_key=workflow_key,
            name=f"{category_key} 历史评测机制",
            description="由旧评测合同幂等迁移生成；不自动设为增量当前启用。",
            owner="platform",
            allowed_categories=[category_key],
            created_by=actor,
        ),
        True,
    )


def migrate_category_revision_history(
    db: Session, category_key: str, *, actor: str
) -> CategoryRevisionHistoryMigrationResult:
    component_result = migrate_category_component_history(
        db, category_key, actor=actor
    )
    revisions = db.scalars(
        select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision)
        .where(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == category_key)
        .order_by(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.revision.asc(),
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.id.asc(),
        )
    ).all()
    definition, _ = _history_definition(db, category_key, actor=actor)
    workflow_versions_created = 0
    mappings_created = 0
    mapping_ids: dict[int, int] = {}
    for revision in revisions:
        existing_mapping = db.scalar(
            select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
                LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id == revision.id
            )
        )
        if existing_mapping is not None:
            mapping_ids[revision.id] = existing_mapping.id
            continue
        version_key = f"legacy-r{revision.revision}"
        workflow_version = db.scalar(
            select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id,
                WorkflowVersion.version == version_key,
            )
        )
        if workflow_version is None:
            from .workflow_working_copies import (
                create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version,
            )

            workflow_version = create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version=version_key,
                manifest=_legacy_revision_manifest(
                    category_key=category_key,
                    revision_id=revision.id,
                    module_versions=component_result.revision_slices[revision.id],
                ),
                created_by=actor,
            )
            workflow_versions_created += 1
        report = component_result.compatibility_reports[revision.id]
        mapping = LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping(
            legacy_revision_id=revision.id,
            workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
            module_versions_json=canonical_json(
                component_result.revision_slices[revision.id]
            ),
            compatibility_report_json=canonical_json(asdict(report)),
            validator_version="legacy-split-v1",
            migration_batch_key=f"modular-evaluation-v1:{category_key}",
        )
        db.add(mapping)
        db.flush()
        mapping_ids[revision.id] = mapping.id
        mappings_created += 1
    return CategoryRevisionHistoryMigrationResult(
        category_key=category_key,
        created_counts={
            **component_result.created_counts,
            "workflow_versions": workflow_versions_created,
            "mappings": mappings_created,
        },
        revision_slices=component_result.revision_slices,
        compatibility_reports=component_result.compatibility_reports,
        mapping_ids=mapping_ids,
    )

MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION = "evaluation-package-manifest-v1"
TERMINAL_REGRESSION_STATUSES = frozenset(
    {"passed", "regressed", "failed", "error", "cancelled"}
)
COMPLETED_AUTOMATION_STATUSES = frozenset(
    {"succeeded", "awaiting_release_review"}
)


def _category_pipeline(
    profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    db: Session | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        raw = json.loads(profile.pipeline_config_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        raw = {}
    if not isinstance(raw, dict) or raw.get("schema_version") != "category-pipeline-v1":
        try:
            legacy = json.loads(profile.preprocess_config_json or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            legacy = {}
        raw = legacy_preprocess_to_pipeline(profile.category_key, legacy)
    allowed_dimension_keys = [item["key"] for item in DIMENSION_OPTIONS]
    if (
        db is not None
        and profile.dimension_schema_key
        and profile.dimension_schema_version
    ):
        schema = db.scalar(
            select(DimensionSchema).where(
                DimensionSchema.schema_key == profile.dimension_schema_key,
                DimensionSchema.version == profile.dimension_schema_version,
            )
        )
        if schema is not None:
            try:
                definition = json.loads(schema.definition_json)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                definition = None
            options = dimension_options_from_definition(definition)
            if options:
                allowed_dimension_keys = [item["key"] for item in options]
    try:
        return validate_pipeline_config(
            raw,
            allowed_dimension_keys=allowed_dimension_keys,
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"类目流水线配置损坏：{exc}") from None


class EvaluationPackageCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    package_key: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=160)
    request_key: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=160)
    idempotency_key: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=160)
    category_key: str | None = Field(
        default=None, pattern=r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{2,39}$"
    )
    regression_run_id: int = Field(ge=1)
    automation_run_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    prompt_a_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    prompt_b_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    dimension_schema_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    dimension_route_policy_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    sample_set_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    baseline_strategy_bundle_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    candidate_strategy_bundle_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    metric_snapshot_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    ai_recommendation: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=40)
    change_summary: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=10_000)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_package_key(self) -> "EvaluationPackageCreateRequest":
        keys = [
            value.strip()
            for value in (
                self.package_key,
                self.request_key,
                self.idempotency_key,
            )
            if value is not None and value.strip()
        ]
        if len(set(keys)) > 1:
            raise ValueError("评测包幂等键只能填写一个且不能互相冲突")
        if not keys:
            raise ValueError("创建评测包必须填写 package_key")
        return self

    def normalized_package_key(self) -> str:
        return str(
            self.package_key or self.request_key or self.idempotency_key
        ).strip()

    ai_recommendation: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=40)
    change_summary: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=10_000)

class StockRerunCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    idempotency_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    category_key: str = Field(pattern=r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{2,39}$")
    target_mechanism_release_id: int = Field(ge=1)
    source_mechanism_release_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    asset_ids: list[int] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=5000)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4000)

    @field_validator("idempotency_key", "reason")
    @classmethod
    def normalize_text(cls, value: str) -> str:
        normalized = value.strip()
        if not normalized:
            raise ValueError("字段不得为空白")
        return normalized

    @field_validator("asset_ids")
    @classmethod
    def validate_asset_ids(cls, values: list[int]) -> list[int]:
        if any(isinstance(value, bool) or value < 1 for value in values):
            raise ValueError("asset_ids 只能包含正整数")
        if len(values) != len(set(values)):
            raise ValueError("asset_ids 不得重复")
        return values


def canonical_json(value: Any) -> str:
    """Return byte-stable UTF-8 JSON used by package manifests."""
    try:
        return json.dumps(
            value,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            allow_nan=False,
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("评测包冻结清单包含无法规范化的数据") from exc


def canonical_manifest_hash(manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(manifest).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _loads_object(value: str | None, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        payload = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{label}不是有效 JSON") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise ValueError(f"{label}必须是 JSON 对象")
    return payload


def _iso(value: datetime | None) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    return platform_isoformat(value)


def _secret_safe(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    forbidden = {
        "apikey",
        "authorization",
        "cookie",
        "credentials",
        "encryptedapikey",
        "password",
        "rawresponse",
        "rawresponsea",
        "rawresponseb",
        "rawresponseriskreview",
        "sessiontoken",
    }

    def strip(node: Any) -> Any:
        if isinstance(node, dict):
            safe: dict[str, Any] = {}
            for key, value in node.items():
                normalized = "".join(
                    character
                    for character in str(key).lower()
                    if character.isalnum()
                )
                if (
                    normalized in forbidden
                    or "rawresponse" in normalized
                    or "rawmodeloutput" in normalized
                    or "rawprovideroutput" in normalized
                ):
                    continue
                safe[str(key)] = strip(value)
            return safe
        if isinstance(node, list):
            return [strip(value) for value in node]
        return node

    return safe_strategy_snapshot_payload(strip(payload))


def _prompt_snapshot(prompt: PromptVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    definition = {
        "id": prompt.id,
        "stage": prompt.stage,
        "name": prompt.name,
        "version": prompt.version,
        "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
        "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
        "change_note": prompt.change_note,
    }
    safe = _secret_safe(definition)
    safe["canonical_hash"] = canonical_manifest_hash(safe)
    return safe


def _resolve_prompt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    prompt_id: int,
    stage: str,
    expected_version: str,
) -> PromptVersion:
    prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, prompt_id)
    if prompt is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"{stage} 阶段提示词不存在")
    if prompt.stage != stage or prompt.version != expected_version:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail=f"{stage} 阶段提示词与候选 StrategyBundle 不一致",
        )
    return prompt


def _strategy_snapshot_from_bundle(
    db: Session,
    *,
    bundle: StrategyBundle,
    preferred_snapshot: str | None,
    prompt_a_id: int | None,
    prompt_b_id: int | None,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], PromptVersion, PromptVersion | None]:
    if preferred_snapshot and preferred_snapshot.strip() not in {"", "{}"}:
        snapshot = safe_strategy_snapshot_payload(preferred_snapshot)
        if (
            snapshot.get("bundle_id") != bundle.id
            or snapshot.get("canonical_hash") != bundle.canonical_hash
        ):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="回归冻结策略与 StrategyBundle 身份不一致",
            )
        prompt_a_data = snapshot.get("prompt_a")
        prompt_b_data = snapshot.get("prompt_b")
        if not isinstance(prompt_a_data, dict):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="回归快照缺少 A 阶段提示词")
        resolved_a_id = prompt_a_id or int(prompt_a_data.get("id") or 0)
        resolved_b_id = (
            prompt_b_id
            or (
                int(prompt_b_data.get("id") or 0)
                if isinstance(prompt_b_data, dict)
                else None
            )
        )
    else:
        resolved_a_id = prompt_a_id or 0
        resolved_b_id = prompt_b_id
        snapshot = {}
    if resolved_a_id <= 0:
        matches = db.scalars(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.stage == "A",
                PromptVersion.version == bundle.prompt_a_version,
            )
        ).all()
        if len(matches) != 1:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="候选 StrategyBundle 无法唯一解析 A 阶段提示词",
            )
        resolved_a_id = matches[0].id
    prompt_a = _resolve_prompt(
        db,
        prompt_id=resolved_a_id,
        stage="A",
        expected_version=bundle.prompt_a_version,
    )

    def assert_frozen_prompt(
        prompt: PromptVersion,
        frozen: Any,
        *,
        stage: str,
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(frozen, dict):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=f"回归冻结策略缺少 {stage} 阶段提示词定义",
            )
        live = _secret_safe(
            {
                "id": prompt.id,
                "stage": prompt.stage,
                "name": prompt.name,
                "version": prompt.version,
                "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
                "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
                "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
            }
        )
        if any(frozen.get(key) != value for key, value in live.items()):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=(
                    f"{stage} 阶段提示词已偏离回归冻结策略，"
                    "请重新回归并创建新评测包"
                ),
            )

    if snapshot:
        assert_frozen_prompt(
            prompt_a,
            snapshot.get("prompt_a"),
            stage="A",
        )
    prompt_b: PromptVersion | None = None
    if bundle.prompt_b_version is not None:
        if resolved_b_id is None:
            matches = db.scalars(
                select(PromptVersion).where(
                    PromptVersion.stage == "B",
                    PromptVersion.version == bundle.prompt_b_version,
                )
            ).all()
            if len(matches) != 1:
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=409,
                    detail="候选 StrategyBundle 无法唯一解析 B 阶段提示词",
                )
            resolved_b_id = matches[0].id
        prompt_b = _resolve_prompt(
            db,
            prompt_id=resolved_b_id,
            stage="B",
            expected_version=bundle.prompt_b_version,
        )
        if snapshot:
            assert_frozen_prompt(
                prompt_b,
                snapshot.get("prompt_b"),
                stage="B",
            )
    elif resolved_b_id is not None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail="单提示词 StrategyBundle 不能冻结 B 阶段提示词",
        )
    if not snapshot:
        policy = (
            db.scalar(
                select(SamplingPolicy).where(
                    SamplingPolicy.revision
                    == bundle.sampling_policy_revision
                )
            )
            if bundle.sampling_policy_revision is not None
            else None
        )
        try:
            snapshot = safe_strategy_snapshot_payload(
                build_strategy_snapshot(
                    bundle,
                    prompt_a,
                    prompt_b,
                    policy,
                )
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    return snapshot, prompt_a, prompt_b


def _regression_recommendation(run: PromptRegressionRun) -> str:
    if run.regression_mode == "paired":
        if run.status not in TERMINAL_REGRESSION_STATUSES:
            return "pending"
        return (
            "pass"
            if run.status == "passed" and run.recommendation == "pass"
            else "fail"
        )
    metrics = _loads_object(run.metrics_json, label="回归指标")
    if run.status not in TERMINAL_REGRESSION_STATUSES:
        return "pending"
    return (
        "pass"
        if run.status == "passed"
        and metrics.get("release_gate_passed") is True
        else "fail"
    )


def _regression_snapshot(run: PromptRegressionRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in sorted(run.items, key=lambda value: value.id):
        truth = _loads_object(item.truth_snapshot_json, label="回归真值快照")
        comparison = _loads_object(item.comparison_json, label="回归比较证据")
        baseline = _loads_object(item.baseline_result_json, label="基线结果快照")
        candidate = _loads_object(item.candidate_result_json, label="候选结果快照")
        items.append(
            {
                "id": item.id,
                "sample_item_id": item.sample_item_id,
                "asset_id": item.sample_item.asset_id,
                "asset_name": item.sample_item.asset.original_name,
                "image_url": f"/api/assets/{item.sample_item.asset_id}/file",
                "sample_role": item.sample_role,
                "status": item.status,
                "passed": item.passed,
                "truth_snapshot": truth or None,
                "comparison": comparison,
                "baseline_result": baseline,
                "candidate_result": candidate,
                "baseline_evaluation_id": item.baseline_evaluation_id,
                "candidate_evaluation_id": item.candidate_evaluation_id,
                "evaluation_id": item.evaluation_id,
            }
        )
    return _secret_safe(
        {
            "id": run.id,
            "name": run.name,
            "mode": run.regression_mode,
            "status": run.status,
            "terminal": run.status in TERMINAL_REGRESSION_STATUSES,
            "recommendation": _regression_recommendation(run),
            "threshold": run.threshold,
            "total": run.total,
            "completed": run.completed,
            "passed": run.passed,
            "failed": run.failed,
            "sample_set_version": run.sample_set_version,
            "sample_manifest": _loads_object(
                run.sample_manifest_json, label="回归黄金集清单"
            ),
            "metric_rules_version": run.metric_rules_version,
            "metric_rules": _loads_object(
                run.metric_rules_json, label="回归指标规则"
            ),
            "metrics": _loads_object(run.metrics_json, label="回归指标"),
            "summary": _loads_object(run.summary_json, label="回归摘要"),
            "legacy_paired_approval": {
                "status": run.approval_status,
                "approved_by": run.approved_by,
                "approval_note": run.approval_note,
                "approved_at": _iso(run.approved_at),
                "release_authority": False,
            },
            "created_by": run.created_by,
            "created_at": _iso(run.created_at),
            "finished_at": _iso(run.finished_at),
            "items": items,
        }
    )


def _golden_sample_snapshot(
    sample_set: SampleSet,
    regression: PromptRegressionRun,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    frozen_manifest = _loads_object(
        regression.sample_manifest_json, label="回归黄金集清单"
    )
    frozen_by_item = {
        int(item.get("sample_item_id")): item
        for item in frozen_manifest.get("items", [])
        if isinstance(item, dict)
        and isinstance(item.get("sample_item_id"), int)
    }
    roles = {item.sample_item_id: item.sample_role for item in regression.items}
    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in sorted(sample_set.items, key=lambda value: value.id):
        frozen = frozen_by_item.get(item.id, {})
        truth_snapshot = frozen.get("truth_snapshot")
        truth = (
            truth_snapshot.get("truth")
            if isinstance(truth_snapshot, dict)
            else _loads_object(item.truth_json, label="黄金样本真值")
        )
        items.append(
            {
                "sample_item_id": item.id,
                "asset_id": item.asset_id,
                "asset_name": item.asset.original_name,
                "asset_sha256": item.asset.sha256,
                "mime_type": item.asset.mime_type,
                "image_url": f"/api/assets/{item.asset_id}/file",
                "role": frozen.get("role") or roles.get(item.id),
                "expected_level": item.expected_level,
                "expected_category": item.expected_category,
                "truth_revision": frozen.get("truth_revision", item.truth_revision),
                "truth": truth or {},
                "source_evaluation_id": item.source_result_id,
            }
        )
    item_manifest_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(items).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return _secret_safe(
        {
            "id": sample_set.id,
            "name": sample_set.name,
            "description": sample_set.description,
            "kind": sample_set.kind,
            "status": sample_set.status,
            "category_key": sample_set.category_key,
            "item_count": len(items),
            "judgable_item_count": sum(
                1 for item in items if bool(item["truth"])
            ),
            "items_manifest_hash": item_manifest_hash,
            "items": items,
        }
    )


def _dimension_snapshot(
    *,
    candidate_strategy: dict[str, Any],
    dimension_schema: DimensionSchema | None,
    route_policy: DimensionRoutePolicy | None,
    category_pipeline: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    explicit_schema = None
    if dimension_schema is not None:
        explicit_schema = {
            "id": dimension_schema.id,
            "schema_key": dimension_schema.schema_key,
            "version": dimension_schema.version,
            "schema_type": dimension_schema.schema_type,
            "family_key": dimension_schema.family_key,
            "display_name": dimension_schema.display_name,
            "status": dimension_schema.status,
            "canonical_hash": dimension_schema.canonical_hash,
            "definition": _loads_object(
                dimension_schema.definition_json,
                label="维度 Schema 定义",
            ),
        }
    explicit_route = None
    if route_policy is not None:
        explicit_route = {
            "id": route_policy.id,
            "policy_key": route_policy.policy_key,
            "version": route_policy.version,
            "display_name": route_policy.display_name,
            "status": route_policy.status,
            "canonical_hash": route_policy.canonical_hash,
            "definition": _loads_object(
                route_policy.definition_json,
                label="维度路由策略定义",
            ),
        }
    explicit_definition = (
        explicit_schema.get("definition")
        if isinstance(explicit_schema, dict)
        else None
    )
    category_selection = dimension_selection_payload(
        category_pipeline,
        dimension_options=(
            dimension_options_from_definition(explicit_definition)
            if isinstance(explicit_definition, dict)
            else list(DIMENSION_OPTIONS)
        ) or list(DIMENSION_OPTIONS),
        schema_key=(dimension_schema.schema_key if dimension_schema else None),
        schema_version=(dimension_schema.version if dimension_schema else None),
        schema_hash=(dimension_schema.canonical_hash if dimension_schema else None),
    )
    return _secret_safe(
        {
            "strategy_schema_version": candidate_strategy.get("schema_version"),
            "resolved_schema_contract_version": candidate_strategy.get(
                "resolved_schema_contract_version"
            ),
            "route_policy_id": candidate_strategy.get(
                "dimension_route_policy_id"
            ),
            "route_policy_snapshot": candidate_strategy.get(
                "dimension_route_policy_snapshot"
