    ) -> ProviderHttpResponse: ...


def _as_utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def _now_utc(factory: Callable[[], datetime]) -> datetime:
    return _as_utc(factory())


def _cache_key(provider: str, version: str, digest: str) -> str:
    contract = {
        "schema_version": "image-first-seen-cache-v1",
        "provider": provider,
        "provider_version": version,
        "image_digest": digest,
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(contract, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _validate_image(image_digest: str, image_bytes: bytes) -> str:
    if not isinstance(image_bytes, bytes) or not image_bytes:
        raise FirstSeenProviderError("image_bytes_invalid", "图片查询内容无效")
    normalized = str(image_digest or "").strip().lower()
    if _DIGEST.fullmatch(normalized) is None:
        raise FirstSeenProviderError("image_digest_invalid", "图片查询摘要无效")
    if hashlib.sha256(image_bytes).hexdigest() != normalized:
        raise FirstSeenProviderError(
            "image_digest_mismatch",
            "图片查询内容与冻结摘要不一致",
        )
    return normalized


def _parse_datetime(value: object) -> datetime | None:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        return None
    normalized = value.strip()
    if normalized.endswith("Z"):
        normalized = normalized[:-1] + "+00:00"
    try:
        return _as_utc(datetime.fromisoformat(normalized))
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _tineye_discovery_dates(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[datetime]:
    results = payload.get("results")
    if isinstance(results, Mapping):
        matches = results.get("matches")
    else:
        matches = payload.get("matches")
    if not isinstance(matches, list):
        return []
    dates: list[datetime] = []
    for match in matches:
        if not isinstance(match, Mapping):
            continue
        backlinks = match.get("backlinks")
        candidates = backlinks if isinstance(backlinks, list) else [match]
        for candidate in candidates:
            if not isinstance(candidate, Mapping):
                continue
            for key in (
                "crawl_date",
                "first_seen_at",
                "first_seen",
                "date",
            ):
                parsed = _parse_datetime(candidate.get(key))
                if parsed is not None:
                    dates.append(parsed)
                    break
    return dates


def _response_hash(response: ProviderHttpResponse) -> str:
    try:
        canonical = json.dumps(
            {
                "status_code": response.status_code,
                "payload": response.payload,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise FirstSeenProviderError(
            "provider_response_invalid",
            "图片查询供应商返回了无法验证的结果",
        ) from exc
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


class UnconfiguredImageFirstSeenProvider:
    def lookup(self, image_digest: str, image_bytes: bytes) -> FirstSeenResult:
        digest = _validate_image(image_digest, image_bytes)
        return FirstSeenResult(
            status="unknown",
            earliest_discovered_at=None,
            provider="unconfigured",
            evidence_count=0,
            cache_key=_cache_key("unconfigured", "none", digest),
        )


class TinEyeImageFirstSeenProvider:
    """TinEye-shaped adapter with injected transport and no persisted URLs."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        db: Session,
        *,
        provider_key: str,
        provider_version: str,
        secret_reference: str,
        quota_pool_id: int,
        transport: FirstSeenTransport,
        secret_resolver: Callable[[str], str] = unprotect_secret,
        timeout_seconds: int = 30,
        now: Callable[[], datetime] = lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    ) -> None:
        provider_key = str(provider_key or "").strip().lower()
        provider_version = str(provider_version or "").strip().lower()
        if (
            _SAFE_KEY.fullmatch(provider_key) is None
            or _SAFE_KEY.fullmatch(provider_version) is None
        ):
            raise FirstSeenProviderError(
                "provider_identity_invalid",
                "图片查询供应商身份无效",
            )
        if not isinstance(secret_reference, str) or not secret_reference.strip():
            raise FirstSeenProviderError(
                "provider_secret_reference_invalid",
                "图片查询供应商凭据引用无效",
            )
        if not isinstance(quota_pool_id, int) or quota_pool_id < 1:
            raise FirstSeenProviderError(
                "provider_quota_pool_invalid",
                "图片查询供应商额度池无效",
            )
        if not isinstance(timeout_seconds, int) or not 1 <= timeout_seconds <= 300:
            raise FirstSeenProviderError(
                "provider_timeout_invalid",
                "图片查询供应商超时必须在 1 到 300 秒之间",
            )
        self._db = db
        self._provider_key = provider_key
        self._provider_version = provider_version
        self._secret_reference = secret_reference
        self._quota_pool_id = quota_pool_id
        self._transport = transport
        self._secret_resolver = secret_resolver
        self._timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds
        self._now = now

    def _cached(self, digest: str) -> FirstSeenResult | None:
        row = self._db.scalar(
            select(ImageFirstSeenCache).where(
                ImageFirstSeenCache.provider_key == self._provider_key,
                ImageFirstSeenCache.provider_version == self._provider_version,
                ImageFirstSeenCache.image_digest == digest,
            )
        )
        if row is None:
            return None
        return FirstSeenResult(
            status=row.status,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            earliest_discovered_at=(
                _as_utc(row.earliest_discovered_at)
                if row.earliest_discovered_at is not None
                else None
            ),
            provider=row.provider_key,
            evidence_count=row.evidence_count,
            cache_key=_cache_key(
                row.provider_key,
                row.provider_version,
                row.image_digest,
            ),
        )

    def _persist(
        self,
        *,
        digest: str,
        status: FirstSeenStatus,
        earliest: datetime | None,
        evidence_count: int,
        response_hash: str,
    ) -> FirstSeenResult:
        row = ImageFirstSeenCache(
            provider_key=self._provider_key,
            provider_version=self._provider_version,
            image_digest=digest,
            status=status,
            earliest_discovered_at=earliest,
            evidence_count=evidence_count,
            response_hash=response_hash,
        )
        self._db.add(row)
        self._db.commit()
        return FirstSeenResult(
            status=status,
            earliest_discovered_at=earliest,
            provider=self._provider_key,
            evidence_count=evidence_count,
            cache_key=_cache_key(
                self._provider_key,
                self._provider_version,
                digest,
            ),
        )

    def lookup(self, image_digest: str, image_bytes: bytes) -> FirstSeenResult:
        digest = _validate_image(image_digest, image_bytes)
        cached = self._cached(digest)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        try:
            api_key = self._secret_resolver(self._secret_reference)
        except Exception:
            return self._persist(
                digest=digest,
                status="unknown",
                earliest=None,
                evidence_count=0,
                response_hash=hashlib.sha256(b"credential-unavailable").hexdigest(),
            )
        try:
            reservation = acquire_provider_quota(
                self._db,
                quota_pool_id=self._quota_pool_id,
                owner_key=f"first-seen:{self._provider_key}:{self._provider_version}:{digest}",
                reserved_images=1,
                now=_now_utc(self._now),
            )
            # The lease is durable before any supplier side effect.
            self._db.commit()
        except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
            return self._persist(
                digest=digest,
                status=(
                    "rate_limited"
                    if exc.retry_after_seconds is not None
                    else "unknown"
                ),
                earliest=None,
                evidence_count=0,
                response_hash=hashlib.sha256(exc.code.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
            )

        response: ProviderHttpResponse
        try:
            response = self._transport(
                image_bytes=image_bytes,
                api_key=api_key,
                timeout_seconds=self._timeout_seconds,
            )
        except TimeoutError:
            response = ProviderHttpResponse(status_code=408, payload=None)
        except Exception:
            response = ProviderHttpResponse(status_code=599, payload=None)

        try:
            if response.status_code == 429:
                cool_down_provider_quota(
                    self._db,
                    quota_pool_id=self._quota_pool_id,
                    retry_after_seconds=max(1, response.retry_after_seconds or 60),
                    reason="provider_rate_limited",
                    now=_now_utc(self._now),
                )
            release_provider_quota(
                self._db,
                lease_token=reservation.lease_token,
                now=_now_utc(self._now),
            )
            self._db.commit()
        except Exception:
            self._db.rollback()
            raise FirstSeenProviderError(
                "provider_quota_release_failed",
                "图片查询供应商额度租约无法安全释放",
            )

        response_hash = _response_hash(response)
        if response.status_code == 429:
            status: FirstSeenStatus = "rate_limited"
            dates: list[datetime] = []
        elif response.status_code in {408, 504}:
            status = "timeout"
            dates = []
        elif response.status_code != 200 or not isinstance(response.payload, Mapping):
            status = "failed"
            dates = []
        else:
            dates = _tineye_discovery_dates(response.payload)
            status = "found" if dates else "not_found"
        return self._persist(
            digest=digest,
            status=status,
            earliest=min(dates) if dates else None,
            evidence_count=len(dates),
            response_hash=response_hash,
        )


def first_seen_callback_payload(
    result: FirstSeenResult,
    *,
    checked_at: datetime,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Map provider states into the existing asynchronous node callback contract."""

    status = {
        "found": "found",
        "not_found": "not_found",
        "failed": "failed",
    }.get(result.status, "unavailable")
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "status": status,
        "web_first_seen_at": (
            _as_utc(result.earliest_discovered_at).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
            if result.earliest_discovered_at is not None
            else None
        ),
        # Provider URLs are intentionally excluded from API and audit output.
        "source_url": None,
        "provider": result.provider,
        "match_kind": "exact" if result.status == "found" else "unknown",
        "confidence": 1.0 if result.status == "found" else 0.0,
        "checked_at": _as_utc(checked_at).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z"),
        "evidence": {
            "provider_result_id": result.cache_key,
            "matched_urls_count": result.evidence_count,
            "result_hash": "",
        },
    }
    hash_basis = json.loads(json.dumps(payload))
    hash_basis["evidence"].pop("result_hash", None)
    payload["evidence"]["result_hash"] = hash_manifest(hash_basis)
    return payload

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_admission.py =====
"""Durable automatic continuation for explicitly paused admission events."""
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json

from sqlalchemy import select, func, or_

from .models import (IncrementalAdmissionBacklog, IncrementalRoutingPolicy,
    IncrementalAutomationControl, CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, WorkflowVersion,
    ContentIngressEvent, ContentRecord, MaterialPackage)

MAX_ADMISSIONS_PER_TICK = 100


def defer_paused_event(db, *, event, category_key, workflow_definition_id):
    if db.scalar(select(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.id).where(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.event_id == event.id)) is None:
        db.add(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog(event_id=event.id, category_key=category_key,
                                         workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id))
        db.flush()


def resume_due_admissions(db, *, actor, now=None):
    from .label_governance import route_content_event_to_incremental_package
    from .incremental_automation import automation_paused
    from .incremental_routing import admission_policy
    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    latest = select(IncrementalRoutingPolicy.category_key, IncrementalRoutingPolicy.workflow_definition_id,
        func.max(IncrementalRoutingPolicy.revision).label("revision")).group_by(
        IncrementalRoutingPolicy.category_key, IncrementalRoutingPolicy.workflow_definition_id).subquery()
    rows = db.scalars(select(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog)
        .join(latest, (latest.c.category_key == IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.category_key)
              & (latest.c.workflow_definition_id == IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.workflow_definition_id))
        .join(IncrementalRoutingPolicy, (IncrementalRoutingPolicy.category_key == latest.c.category_key)
              & (IncrementalRoutingPolicy.workflow_definition_id == latest.c.workflow_definition_id)
              & (IncrementalRoutingPolicy.revision == latest.c.revision))
        .where(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.status == "pending", IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.retry_at <= current,
            IncrementalRoutingPolicy.admission_paused.is_(False),
            ~select(IncrementalAutomationControl.id).where(
                IncrementalAutomationControl.category_key == IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.category_key,
                IncrementalAutomationControl.workflow_definition_id == IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.workflow_definition_id,
                # Mirror automation_paused (archived OR disabled). Rows it would
                # skip must not be selected: skipping does not move retry_at, so
                # they filled every 100-row page and starved other scopes.
                or_(IncrementalAutomationControl.enabled.is_(False),
                    IncrementalAutomationControl.archived.is_(True))).exists())
        .order_by(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.retry_at, IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.id)
        .limit(MAX_ADMISSIONS_PER_TICK)).all()
    for row in rows:
        try:
            with db.begin_nested():
                binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
                    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key == row.category_key))
                version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, binding.workflow_version_id) if binding else None
                if version is None or version.workflow_definition_id != row.workflow_definition_id:
                    row.status, row.error_code = "blocked", "routing_scope_changed"
                    continue
                if automation_paused(db, category_key=row.category_key, workflow_version_id=version.id):
                    continue
                # Another worker may have finished this row while we waited
                # for the definition lock. Re-read durable status under lock.
                row = db.scalar(select(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog).where(
                    IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.id == row.id).with_for_update()
                    .execution_options(populate_existing=True))
                if row.status != "pending":
                    continue
                policy = admission_policy(db, category_key=row.category_key, workflow_definition_id=row.workflow_definition_id)
                if policy is None or policy.admission_paused:
                    continue
                event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, row.event_id)
                record = db.get(ContentRecord, event.content_record_id) if event else None
                if record is None:
                    row.status, row.error_code = "blocked", "source_record_missing"
                    continue
                # A newer source event supersedes this unadmitted version. Never
                # attach mutable current material to an older paused payload.
                payload = json.loads(event.payload_json)
                if event.status == "stale" or str(payload.get("content_version")) != record.source_version or record.status == "deleted":
                    row.status = "superseded"
                    continue
                existing = db.scalar(select(MaterialPackage.id).where(MaterialPackage.incremental_event_id == event.id))
                if existing is not None:
                    row.status = "completed"
                    continue
                package, _, routing_status = route_content_event_to_incremental_package(
                    db, event=event, record=record, duplicate=True, actor=actor)
                # Materialize only. The normal dispatcher owns the shared run
                # limit and returns every created run in this worker tick.
                if package is not None or routing_status in {"routing_old", "routing_excluded", "ignored"}:
                    row.status, row.error_code = "completed", None
                else:
                    row.failure_count += 1
                    row.error_code = routing_status[:80]
                    row.retry_at = current + timedelta(seconds=min(300, 5 * 2 ** min(row.failure_count - 1, 6)))
        except Exception as exc:
            # The savepoint keeps one malformed event from rolling back healthy
            # admissions. Store only a code, never provider or source contents.
            row.failure_count += 1
            row.error_code = str(getattr(exc, "code", type(exc).__name__))[:80]
            row.retry_at = current + timedelta(seconds=min(300, 5 * 2 ** min(row.failure_count - 1, 6)))
    db.flush()

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_automation.py =====
"""Workflow-scoped operational control; never edits frozen workflow policy."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json

from sqlalchemy import func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event
from .incremental_input import incremental_autorun_policy
from .incremental_mechanisms import (
    IncrementalMechanismError, require_incremental_compatible_workflow,
    incremental_binding_runtime_context,
)
from .models import (
    AuditEvent, CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    IncrementalAutomationControl, IncrementalAdmissionBacklog, MaterialPackage, ProductionRun,
    WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from .workflow_model_budget import WorkflowModelBudgetError, real_execution_plan_for_manifest


def _control(db: Session, category: str, definition_id: int, *, shared: bool = False):
    return db.scalar(select(IncrementalAutomationControl).where(
        IncrementalAutomationControl.category_key == category,
        IncrementalAutomationControl.workflow_definition_id == definition_id,
    ).with_for_update(read=shared).execution_options(populate_existing=True))


def _lock_definition(db: Session, definition_id: int, *, shared: bool = False):
    if db.get_bind().dialect.name == "sqlite":
        # SQLite ignores FOR UPDATE and its legacy driver does not begin a
        # transaction on SELECT. Acquire the write lock without changing any
        # workflow fields, so pause and dispatch have a defined commit order.
        db.execute(update(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.id == definition_id)
            .values(updated_at=WorkflowDefinition.updated_at)
            .execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
    return db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
        WorkflowDefinition.id == definition_id,
    ).with_for_update(read=shared))


def automation_paused(db: Session, *, category_key: str, workflow_version_id: int) -> bool:
    """Shared lock with switch updates, held through the dispatch transaction.

    Missing controls preserve the pre-upgrade policy. This only gates new
    automatic runs; already queued runs and manual stock runs are unaffected.
    """
    version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_version_id)
    if version is None:
        return True
    # Readers coexist on row-locking databases; switch writers still acquire
    # the exclusive parent fence, including when a control row does not exist.
    # SQLite retains its existing transaction-wide write fence above.
    _lock_definition(db, version.workflow_definition_id, shared=True)
    control = _control(db, category_key, version.workflow_definition_id, shared=True)
    return control is not None and (control.archived or not control.enabled)


def set_automation_control(db: Session, *, category_key: str, workflow_definition_id: int,
                           enabled: bool, expected_revision: int, reason: str, actor: str) -> dict:
    if not reason.strip():
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_reason_required", "请填写操作原因", status_code=422)
    definition = _lock_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    if definition is None:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_not_found", "工作流不存在", status_code=404)
    cards = automation_cards(db, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id)["items"]
    card = next((c for c in cards if c["category_key"] == category_key and c["workflow_definition_id"] == workflow_definition_id), None)
    if card is None:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_not_found", "该类目没有此增量工作流", status_code=404)
    current = _control(db, category_key, workflow_definition_id)
    if enabled and current and current.archived:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_archived", "增量链路已归档，请先恢复", status_code=409)
    revision = current.revision if current else 0
    if revision != expected_revision:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_conflict", "开关已被其他操作更新，请刷新后重试", status_code=409)
    if enabled and card["enable_blocker"]:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_not_ready", card["enable_blocker"], status_code=409)
    if enabled:
        require_incremental_compatible_workflow(db, category_key=category_key, workflow_version_id=card["workflow_version_id"])
    before = card["enabled"]
    if current is None:
        db.add(IncrementalAutomationControl(category_key=category_key,
            workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id, enabled=enabled,
            revision=1, reason=reason.strip(), updated_by=actor))
    else:
        changed = db.execute(update(IncrementalAutomationControl).where(
            IncrementalAutomationControl.id == current.id,
            IncrementalAutomationControl.revision == expected_revision,
        ).values(enabled=enabled, revision=revision + 1, reason=reason.strip(), updated_by=actor,
                 updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)).execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
        if changed.rowcount != 1:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_conflict", "开关已变化，请刷新", status_code=409)
    db.flush()
    append_audit_event(db, category="incremental_automation", action="enable" if enabled else "pause",
        subject_type="workflow_definition", subject_id=workflow_definition_id, actor=actor,
        payload={"category_key": category_key, "before_enabled": before, "enabled": enabled,
                 "revision": revision + 1, "reason": reason.strip()},
        event_key=f"incremental-automation:{category_key}:{workflow_definition_id}:{revision + 1}")
    return {"enabled": enabled, "revision": revision + 1}


def archive_automation_control(db: Session, *, category_key: str, workflow_definition_id: int,
                               archived: bool, expected_revision: int, reason: str, actor: str) -> dict:
    """Archive only the incremental lane; retain stock versions and history."""
    _lock_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    current = _control(db, category_key, workflow_definition_id)
    if current is None or current.revision != expected_revision:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_conflict", "增量链路不存在或修订已变化", status_code=409)
    if not reason.strip():
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_reason_required", "请填写操作原因", status_code=422)
    if archived:
        binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
            CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key == category_key))
        version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, binding.workflow_version_id) if binding else None
        if version and version.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_bound", "当前正式增量链路不能归档", status_code=409)
        from .incremental_routing import current_policy
        policy = current_policy(db, category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id)
        if current.enabled or (policy and not policy.admission_paused):
            raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_not_paused", "请先暂停此链路的受理与派发", status_code=409)
        active = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun).where(
            ProductionRun.category_key == category_key, ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id,
            ProductionRun.run_mode == "incremental", ProductionRun.status.not_in(("succeeded", "failed", "canceled"))))
        if active:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_not_drained", "此增量链路仍有未结束运行", status_code=409)
        pending = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(MaterialPackage).join(
            WorkflowVersion, MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id == WorkflowVersion.id).where(
            MaterialPackage.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id,
            MaterialPackage.source == "production_import",
            MaterialPackage.status.in_(("incremental_pending", "incremental_blocked"))))
        backlog = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog).where(
            IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.category_key == category_key,
            IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id,
            IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.status == "pending"))
        if pending or backlog:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError("automation_not_drained", "此增量链路仍有待处置素材或受理积压", status_code=409)
    current.archived = archived
    current.revision += 1
    current.updated_by, current.reason = actor, reason.strip()
    db.flush()
    append_audit_event(db, category="incremental_automation", action="archive" if archived else "restore",
        subject_type="workflow_definition", subject_id=workflow_definition_id, actor=actor,
        payload={"category_key": category_key, "archived": archived, "revision": current.revision, "reason": reason.strip()},
        event_key=f"incremental-automation:{category_key}:{workflow_definition_id}:{current.revision}")
    return {"archived": archived, "enabled": current.enabled, "revision": current.revision}


def set_automation_operation(db: Session, *, category_key: str, workflow_definition_id: int,
                             enabled: bool, expected_revision: int, expected_routing_revision: int,
                             reason: str, actor: str) -> dict:
    """One transaction controls admission and dispatch; caller commits both."""
    from .incremental_routing import current_policy, set_admission
    scope = dict(category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id)
    _lock_definition(db, workflow_definition_id)
    policy = current_policy(db, **scope, current_read=True)
    if expected_routing_revision != (policy.revision if policy else 0):
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("routing_revision_conflict", "受理策略已变化，请刷新后重试", status_code=409)
    result = set_automation_control(db, **scope, enabled=enabled, expected_revision=expected_revision,
                                    reason=reason, actor=actor)
    if policy is not None:
        policy_result = set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=expected_routing_revision,
                                     paused=not enabled, reason=reason, actor=actor)
        result["routing_revision"] = policy_result["revision"]
    else:
        result["routing_revision"] = 0
    return result


def _service_class_fields(db: Session, category: str, binding) -> dict:
    """卡片上要显示这个类目**实际**在用的优先级，不是绑定表上填没填。

    ``resolve_service_class`` 是绑定表优先、部署清单其次。只读绑定表的话，靠
    ``INCREMENTAL_SLA_HIGH_CATEGORIES`` 拿到高优先的类目会被显示成「普通」——
    2026-09-16 线上 model_3d_su 正是如此：最近 1016 条运行全部冻结成 high，卡片
    却写着「普通（沿用部署默认，尚未在此设置）」。灰度之外再接高量类目时，运营
    正是拿这个页面判断谁先拿提交名额，显示错了会直接误导。
    """

    from .incremental_sla import configured_service_class, enabled, resolve_service_class

    explicit = configured_service_class(db, category)
    if explicit is not None:
        return {"service_class": explicit, "service_class_source": "category"}
    if not enabled():
        # 功能没开，所有素材一视同仁；说「普通」会让人以为设过。
        return {"service_class": None, "service_class_source": "sla_disabled"}
    try:
        return {"service_class": resolve_service_class(db, category),
                "service_class_source": "deployment_default"}
    except ValueError:
        # 开了却没给清单：部署侧没说话，照实报，不要替它假定普通。
        return {"service_class": None, "service_class_source": "deployment_unset"}


def automation_cards(db: Session, *, workflow_definition_id: int | None = None,
    include_costs: bool = True) -> dict:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    since = now - timedelta(hours=24)
    categories = {c.category_key: c.display_name for c in db.scalars(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile)).all()}
    binding_rows = {b.category_key: b for b in db.scalars(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)).all()}
    bindings = {category: binding.workflow_version_id for category, binding in binding_rows.items()}
    definitions = {d.id: d for d in db.scalars(select(WorkflowDefinition)).all()}
    controls = {(c.category_key, c.workflow_definition_id): c for c in db.scalars(select(IncrementalAutomationControl)).all()}
    selected = {}
    versions = select(WorkflowVersion).where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3").order_by(WorkflowVersion.id)
    if workflow_definition_id is not None:
        versions = versions.where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id)
    for version in db.scalars(versions).all():
        if workflow_definition_id is None and definitions[version.workflow_definition_id].status != "active":
            continue
        manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
        category = manifest.get("category_key")
        if not category or "incremental" not in manifest.get("run_modes", []):
            continue
        key = (category, version.workflow_definition_id)
        if workflow_definition_id is None and controls.get(key) and controls[key].archived:
            continue
        previous = selected.get(key)
        if previous and bindings.get(category) == previous[0].id:
            continue
        selected[key] = (version, manifest)
    # Only native content ingress runs count; stock/manual production is separate.
    run_scope = ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event"
    recent = {}
    for category, definition_id, status, count in db.execute(select(
        ProductionRun.category_key, ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id, ProductionRun.status, func.count(),
    ).where(run_scope, ProductionRun.created_at >= since).group_by(
        ProductionRun.category_key, ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id, ProductionRun.status,
    )):
        recent.setdefault((category, definition_id), {})[status] = count
    active = {}
    for category, definition_id, count in db.execute(select(
        ProductionRun.category_key, ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id, func.count(),
    ).where(run_scope, ProductionRun.status.in_(["planned", "queued", "running", "waiting_external", "paused", "retryable"])).group_by(
        ProductionRun.category_key, ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id,
    )):
        active[(category, definition_id)] = count
    pending = {}
    for category, definition_id, count, oldest in db.execute(select(
        MaterialPackage.category_key, WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id, func.count(), func.min(MaterialPackage.created_at),
    ).join(WorkflowVersion, MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id == WorkflowVersion.id).where(
        MaterialPackage.source == "production_import", MaterialPackage.status == "incremental_pending",
    ).group_by(MaterialPackage.category_key, WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id)):
        pending[(category, definition_id)] = (count, oldest)
    latest = {}
    blocked = dict(((category, definition_id), count) for category, definition_id, count in db.execute(
        select(MaterialPackage.category_key, WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id, func.count())
        .join(WorkflowVersion, WorkflowVersion.id == MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id)
        .where(MaterialPackage.source == "production_import", MaterialPackage.status == "incremental_blocked")
        .group_by(MaterialPackage.category_key, WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id)
    ))
    ranked_blocked = select(
        MaterialPackage.id.label("package_id"), MaterialPackage.category_key,
        WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id, MaterialPackage.incremental_lane_key,
        MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id,
        func.row_number().over(partition_by=(MaterialPackage.category_key, WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id),
            order_by=MaterialPackage.id.desc()).label("rank"),
    ).join(WorkflowVersion, WorkflowVersion.id == MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id).where(
        MaterialPackage.source == "production_import", MaterialPackage.status == "incremental_blocked",
    ).subquery()
    blocked_examples = {}
    for row in db.execute(select(ranked_blocked, AuditEvent.payload_json).outerjoin(AuditEvent,
        (AuditEvent.subject_id == ranked_blocked.c.incremental_lane_key)
        & (AuditEvent.action == "incremental_lane_blocked")
        & (AuditEvent.category == "content_ingress"),
    ).where(ranked_blocked.c.rank <= 5).order_by(ranked_blocked.c.rank)).mappings():
        try:
            evidence = json.loads(row["payload_json"] or "{}")
            code = evidence.get("error_code", "unknown") if isinstance(evidence, dict) else "unknown"
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            code = "unknown"
        blocked_examples.setdefault((row["category_key"], row["workflow_definition_id"]), []).append({
            "package_id": row["package_id"], "workflow_version_id": row["workflow_version_id"], "error_code": code,
        })
    received = dict(((category, definition_id), count) for category, definition_id, count in db.execute(
        select(MaterialPackage.category_key, WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id, func.count())
        .join(WorkflowVersion, WorkflowVersion.id == MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id)
        .where(MaterialPackage.incremental_event_id.is_not(None), MaterialPackage.created_at >= since)
        .group_by(MaterialPackage.category_key, WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id)
    ))
    recent_runs = {}
    # Only the displayed lanes need a preview. Ranking every historical run
    # on every refresh becomes progressively slower as intake grows.
    for category, definition_id in selected:
        scope = (run_scope, ProductionRun.category_key == category,
            ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id == definition_id)
        latest[(category, definition_id)] = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun.created_at)
            .where(*scope).order_by(ProductionRun.created_at.desc()).limit(1))
        recent_runs[(category, definition_id)] = [dict(row) for row in db.execute(select(
            ProductionRun.run_key, ProductionRun.workflow_version_id,
            ProductionRun.status, ProductionRun.error_message, ProductionRun.created_at,
        ).where(*scope).order_by(ProductionRun.id.desc()).limit(5)).mappings()]
    items = []
    admission_backlogs = {(category, definition): count for category, definition, count in db.execute(
        select(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.category_key, IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.workflow_definition_id, func.count())
        .where(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.status == "pending")
        .group_by(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.category_key, IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.workflow_definition_id))}
    for key, (version, manifest) in selected.items():
        category, definition_id = key
        definition = definitions[definition_id]
        control = controls.get(key)
        bound = bindings.get(category) == version.id
        try:
            policy = incremental_autorun_policy({
                "workflow": {"manifest": manifest},
                "runtime_context": incremental_binding_runtime_context(binding_rows[category]) if bound else {},
            })
            policy_error = None
        except ValueError as exc:
            policy = {"enabled": False, "model_execution_mode": "simulated"}
            policy_error = str(exc)
        blocker = policy_error or ("请在工作流管理中绑定该工作流为当前增量版本" if not bound else
            "工作流已停用" if definition.status != "active" else
            "冻结工作流未开启自动组批，请在工作流管理中配置并生成版本" if not policy["enabled"] else None)
        try:
            report = json.loads(version.validation_report_json or "{}")
            if not isinstance(report, dict) or report.get("ok") is not True:
                blocker = blocker or "工作流检查尚未通过，请前往工作流管理处理"
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            blocker = blocker or "工作流检查记录损坏，请前往工作流管理处理"
        if not blocker and policy["model_execution_mode"] == "real":
            try:
                # Read-only readiness check. Actual dispatch still freezes and
                # enforces its own per-event input budget and authorization.
                from .doubao_endpoints import select_inference_route, DoubaoEndpointError
                route = select_inference_route(db, definition.id, offline=True)
                real_execution_plan_for_manifest(db, manifest, inference_route=route)
            except (WorkflowModelBudgetError, DoubaoEndpointError) as exc:
                blocker = str(exc)
        # The independent gate is open unless explicitly paused. Readiness of
        # the displayed version cannot describe every historical frozen lane:
        # older versions may still have enabled, dispatchable work.
        enabled = control.enabled if control else True
        counts = recent.get(key, {})
        pending_count, oldest = pending.get(key, (0, None))
        if oldest and oldest.tzinfo is None:
            oldest = oldest.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        nodes = manifest.get("nodes", [])
        from .incremental_routing import routing_status
        from .incremental_observability import cost_summary, waiting_material_count
        waiting_material = waiting_material_count(db, category) if bound else 0
        routing = routing_status(db, category_key=category, workflow_definition_id=definition_id)
        from .incremental_readiness import automation_readiness_blocker
        blocker = blocker or automation_readiness_blocker(
            db, manifest, routing["policy"], model_execution_mode=policy['model_execution_mode'])
        from .doubao_endpoints import endpoint_readiness_blocker, endpoint_route_summary
        blocker = blocker or endpoint_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, definition_id)
        # 卡片要显示这一轮真正会打的那条，不是绑定表上的在线位——见
        # ``endpoint_route_summary`` 的注释。
        model_snapshots = [node.get("config", {}).get("model_snapshot", {}) for node in nodes if node.get("kind") == "prompt"]
        local_models = sorted({snapshot.get("model_id", "Qwen") for snapshot in model_snapshots if snapshot.get("provider") == "local_qwen"})
        all_local = bool(model_snapshots) and all(snapshot.get("provider") == "local_qwen" for snapshot in model_snapshots)
        doubao_route = None if all_local else endpoint_route_summary(db, definition_id)
        operation_requested = bool(enabled) and not (routing["policy"] and routing["policy"]["admission_paused"])
        operation_enabled = operation_requested and not bool(blocker)
        items.append({
            "category_key": category, "category_name": categories.get(category, category),
            "workflow_definition_id": definition_id, "workflow_key": definition.workflow_key,
            "workflow_name": definition.name, "workflow_version_id": version.id, "workflow_version": version.version,
            "bound": bound, "enabled": bool(enabled), "revision": control.revision if control else 0,
            "operation_enabled": operation_enabled,
            "control_source": "explicit" if control else "default_open",
            "enable_blocker": blocker,
            "state": "paused" if not operation_requested else "attention" if blocked.get(key) or waiting_material else "blocked" if blocker else "active" if active.get(key) else "waiting",
            "execution_mode": policy["model_execution_mode"],
            "local_models": local_models if all_local else [],
            # Category-level queue priority. Carried on every card of the
            # category because that is where an operator looks; the mutation
            # itself is per category and guarded by mechanism_lock_version.
            **_service_class_fields(db, category, binding_rows.get(category)),
            "mechanism_lock_version": getattr(binding_rows.get(category), "lock_version", None),
            "upstream": [n.get("display_name", "读取上游") for n in nodes if n.get("kind") == "upstream_connector"],
            "downstream": [n.get("display_name", "写入下游") for n in nodes if n.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"],
            "routing_status": routing["policy"]["mode"] if routing["policy"] else "not_configured",
            "routing": routing,
            "costs": cost_summary(db, category_key=category, workflow_definition_id=definition_id) if include_costs else None,
            "doubao_route": doubao_route,
            "last_run_at": latest.get(key),
            "recent_runs": recent_runs.get(key, []),
            "blocked_examples": blocked_examples.get(key, []),
            "metrics": {"received": received.get(key, 0), "runs": sum(counts.values()), "succeeded": counts.get("succeeded", 0),
                "admission_backlog": admission_backlogs.get(key, 0),
                "blocked_inputs": blocked.get(key, 0),
                "waiting_material": waiting_material,
                "failed": counts.get("failed", 0) + counts.get("blocked", 0), "in_flight": active.get(key, 0),
                "pending": pending_count, "oldest_wait_seconds": int((now - oldest).total_seconds()) if oldest else None},
        })
    return {"items": items, "updated_at": now, "window_start": since, "window_hours": 24}

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_autorun.py =====
"""Idempotent content-ingress to native incremental runtime dispatch.

The content ingress API already owns source identity and materialisation.  This
module closes the remaining gap: once a ready local asset and a frozen category
mechanism exist, it creates the runtime run in the same transaction.  No remote
database is queried here and no credential is copied into the run snapshot.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text

import hashlib
import json
from collections import deque
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import func, or_, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .label_governance import route_content_event_to_incremental_package
from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat
from .incremental_input import (
    IncrementalInputError,
    frozen_input_hash,
    incremental_autorun_policy,
    incremental_lane_key,
)
from .models import (
    ContentIngressEvent,
    ContentRecord,
    MaterialPackage,
    ProductionRun,
    IncrementalAutomationControl,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_model_budget import (
    WorkflowModelBudgetError,
    real_execution_plan_for_manifest,
)
from .workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    WorkflowRuntimeError,
    create_production_run,
)


class IncrementalAutorunError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


MAX_INCREMENTAL_LANES_PER_TICK = 100
MAX_INCREMENTAL_RUNS_PER_TICK = 1_000
INITIAL_INCREMENTAL_RETRY_SECONDS = 5
MAX_INCREMENTAL_RETRY_SECONDS = 300
PERMANENT_INCREMENTAL_LANE_ERROR_CODES = frozenset(
    {
        "content_ingress_binding_mismatch",
        "content_ingress_run_conflict",
        "incremental_autorun_policy_invalid",
        "incremental_batch_lane_mismatch",
        "incremental_batch_source_mismatch",
        "incremental_batch_source_missing",
        "incremental_snapshot_invalid",
        "incremental_snapshot_mismatch",
        "incremental_upstream_contract_missing",
    }
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IncrementalDispatchResult:
    package_id: int | None
    package_created: bool
    batch_key: str | None
    run_key: str | None
    run_created: bool
    routing_status: str

    @property
    def writes_evaluation_job(self) -> bool:
        return self.run_key is not None


def _json_object(value: str, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_snapshot_invalid",
            f"{label}损坏",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_snapshot_invalid",
            f"{label}不是对象",
        )
    return parsed


def _mechanism_snapshot(package: MaterialPackage) -> dict[str, Any]:
    snapshot = _json_object(package.mechanism_snapshot_json, label="增量机制快照")
    if (
        snapshot.get("schema_version")
        != "category-incremental-mechanism-snapshot-v1"
        or not isinstance(snapshot.get("workflow_version_id"), int)
        or not isinstance(snapshot.get("snapshot_hash"), str)
        or len(snapshot["snapshot_hash"]) != 64
        or not isinstance(snapshot.get("workflow_snapshot"), Mapping)
        or not isinstance(snapshot.get("input_manifest"), Mapping)
        or not isinstance(snapshot.get("input_manifest_hash"), str)
        or frozen_input_hash(snapshot["input_manifest"])
        != snapshot["input_manifest_hash"]
    ):
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_snapshot_invalid",
            "增量机制快照缺少冻结版本或哈希",
        )
    if package.workflow_version_id != snapshot["workflow_version_id"]:
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_snapshot_mismatch",
            "素材包与冻结增量机制版本不一致",
        )
    return snapshot


def _frozen_upstream_contract(snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, str]:
    workflow_snapshot = snapshot.get("workflow_snapshot")
    workflow = (
        workflow_snapshot.get("workflow")
        if isinstance(workflow_snapshot, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    for node in nodes or []:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "upstream_connector":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        connector_snapshot = (
            config.get("connector_snapshot") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        )
        if not isinstance(connector_snapshot, Mapping):
            continue
        contract_id = connector_snapshot.get("connector_contract_id")
        contract_key = connector_snapshot.get("contract_key")
        if isinstance(contract_id, int) and isinstance(contract_key, str) and contract_key:
            return contract_id, contract_key
    raise IncrementalAutorunError(
        "incremental_upstream_contract_missing",
        "冻结增量机制缺少上游读取合同",
    )


def _autorun_policy(snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    workflow_snapshot = snapshot.get("workflow_snapshot")
    if not isinstance(workflow_snapshot, Mapping):
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_snapshot_invalid",
            "增量机制快照缺少冻结工作流",
        )
    try:
        return incremental_autorun_policy(workflow_snapshot)
    except IncrementalInputError as exc:
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_autorun_policy_invalid",
            str(exc),
        ) from exc


def _real_execution_plan(
    db: Session,
    *,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    include_route: bool = False,
) -> tuple:
    """Freeze one-asset cost and model identity before an automatic paid run."""

    workflow_snapshot = snapshot.get("workflow_snapshot")
    workflow = (
        workflow_snapshot.get("workflow")
        if isinstance(workflow_snapshot, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping):
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_snapshot_invalid",
            "增量机制快照缺少冻结工作流定义",
        )
    from .models import WorkflowVersion
    from .doubao_endpoints import select_inference_route, DoubaoEndpointError
    version_id = snapshot.get("workflow_version_id")
    version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, version_id) if isinstance(version_id, int) else None
    try:
        route = select_inference_route(db, version.workflow_definition_id, offline=True) if version else None
        budget, node_limits = real_execution_plan_for_manifest(
            db,
            manifest,
            inference_route=route,
            input_manifest=(
                snapshot.get("input_manifest")
                if isinstance(snapshot.get("input_manifest"), Mapping)
                else None
            ),
        )
        return (budget, node_limits, route) if include_route else (budget, node_limits)
    except (WorkflowModelBudgetError, DoubaoEndpointError) as exc:
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc


def _bounded_idempotency_key(event_id: str, snapshot_hash: str) -> str:
    raw = f"content-ingress:{event_id}:{snapshot_hash}"
    if len(raw) <= 200:
        return raw
    event_digest = hashlib.sha256(event_id.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:48]
    return f"content-ingress:{event_digest}:{snapshot_hash}"


def _batch_key(event_ids: list[str], snapshot_hash: str) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(
        f"{','.join(sorted(event_ids))}:{snapshot_hash}".encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()[:32]
    return f"content-ingress-batch:{digest}"


def _run_context(run: ProductionRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    snapshot = _json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run), label="运行快照")
    context = snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    return dict(context) if isinstance(context, Mapping) else {}


def _existing_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event: ContentIngressEvent,
    snapshot_hash: str,
) -> ProductionRun | None:
    return db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun).where(
            ProductionRun.idempotency_key
            == _bounded_idempotency_key(event.event_id, snapshot_hash)
        )
    )


def _lane_filter(
    *,
    lane_key: str,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    snapshot_hash: str,
) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
    return (
        MaterialPackage.source == "production_import",
        MaterialPackage.status == "incremental_pending",
        MaterialPackage.incremental_lane_key == lane_key,
        MaterialPackage.category_key == category_key,
        MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id == workflow_version_id,
        MaterialPackage.incremental_snapshot_hash == snapshot_hash,
    )


def _active_lane_backoff(
    db: Session,
    *,
    lane_key: str,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    snapshot_hash: str,
    current: datetime,
) -> tuple[datetime, int] | None:
    """Inherit one lane-wide retry gate onto newly arrived packages."""

    lane_filter = _lane_filter(
        lane_key=lane_key,
        category_key=category_key,
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
    )
    retry_at, failure_count = db.execute(
        select(
            func.max(MaterialPackage.incremental_retry_at),
            func.max(
                func.coalesce(MaterialPackage.incremental_failure_count, 0)
            ),
        ).where(*lane_filter)
    ).one()
    if retry_at is None:
        return None
    retry_at_utc = (
        retry_at
        if retry_at.tzinfo is not None
        else retry_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )
    if current >= retry_at_utc:
        return None
    normalized_failures = max(1, int(failure_count or 0))
    db.execute(
        update(MaterialPackage)
        .where(*lane_filter)
        .values(
            incremental_retry_at=retry_at,
            incremental_failure_count=normalized_failures,
        )
    )
    return retry_at, normalized_failures


def _pending_lane_event_ids(
    db: Session,
    *,
    lane_key: str,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    snapshot_hash: str,
    limit: int,
) -> list[str]:
    return list(
        db.scalars(
            select(ContentIngressEvent.event_id)
            .join(
                MaterialPackage,
                MaterialPackage.incremental_event_id
                == ContentIngressEvent.id,
            )
            .where(
                *_lane_filter(
                    lane_key=lane_key,
                    category_key=category_key,
                    workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
                    snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
                )
            )
            .order_by(MaterialPackage.id.asc())
            .limit(limit)
        ).all()
    )


def _pending_packages(
    db: Session,
    *,
    lane_key: str,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    snapshot_hash: str,
    limit: int,
) -> list[tuple[MaterialPackage, ContentIngressEvent]]:
    rows: list[tuple[MaterialPackage, ContentIngressEvent]] = []
    package_events = db.execute(
        select(MaterialPackage, ContentIngressEvent)
        .join(
            ContentIngressEvent,
            ContentIngressEvent.id == MaterialPackage.incremental_event_id,
        )
        .where(
            *_lane_filter(
                lane_key=lane_key,
                category_key=category_key,
                workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
                snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
            ),
        )
        .order_by(MaterialPackage.created_at.asc(), MaterialPackage.id.asc())
        .limit(limit)
    ).all()
    candidates: list[
        tuple[MaterialPackage, ContentIngressEvent, dict[str, Any]]
    ] = []
    for candidate, candidate_event in package_events:
        candidate_snapshot = _mechanism_snapshot(candidate)
        if (
            candidate_snapshot.get("snapshot_hash") != snapshot_hash
            or candidate.incremental_snapshot_hash != snapshot_hash
            or candidate.incremental_event_id != candidate_event.id
        ):
            raise IncrementalAutorunError(
                "incremental_batch_source_mismatch",
                "自动增量索引事实与冻结快照不一致",
            )
        candidates.append((candidate, candidate_event, candidate_snapshot))
    idempotency_keys = {
        _bounded_idempotency_key(candidate_event.event_id, snapshot_hash)
        for _candidate, candidate_event, _snapshot in candidates
    }
    existing_keys = (
        set(
            db.scalars(
                select(ProductionRun.idempotency_key).where(
                    ProductionRun.idempotency_key.in_(idempotency_keys)
                )
            ).all()
        )
        if idempotency_keys
        else set()
    )
    for candidate, candidate_event, candidate_snapshot in candidates:
        if (
            candidate_snapshot.get("content_record_id")
            != candidate_event.content_record_id
        ):
            raise IncrementalAutorunError(
                "incremental_batch_source_mismatch",
                "自动增量待组批素材与接入事实不一致",
            )
        key = _bounded_idempotency_key(candidate_event.event_id, snapshot_hash)
        if key in existing_keys:
            candidate.status = "incremental_sent"
            continue
        rows.append((candidate, candidate_event))
    return rows


def _create_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    package: MaterialPackage,
    event: ContentIngressEvent,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    policy: Mapping[str, Any],
    batch_key: str,
    trigger_reason: str,
    actor: str,
) -> tuple[ProductionRun, bool]:
    locked_event = db.scalar(
        select(ContentIngressEvent)
        .where(ContentIngressEvent.id == event.id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if locked_event is None:
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_batch_source_missing",
            "自动增量接入事实不存在",
        )
    event = locked_event
    existing = _existing_run(
        db,
        event=event,
        snapshot_hash=str(snapshot["snapshot_hash"]),
    )
    if existing is not None:
        from .connector_native_dispatch import link_connector_run
        link_connector_run(db, canonical=event, run=existing)
        package.status = "incremental_sent"
        return existing, False

    input_manifest = dict(snapshot["input_manifest"])
    input_hash = str(snapshot["input_manifest_hash"])
    contract_id, contract_key = _frozen_upstream_contract(snapshot)
    real_execution = policy["model_execution_mode"] == "real"
    frozen_context = snapshot['workflow_snapshot'].get('runtime_context', {})
    real_budget: dict[str, int] | None = None
    real_node_limits: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None
    # The runtime authorizer derives route and paid limits together from the
    # stored package. Prepricing here would select the route a second time.
    run = create_production_run(
        db,
        ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=int(snapshot["workflow_version_id"]),
            idempotency_key=_bounded_idempotency_key(
                event.event_id,
                str(snapshot["snapshot_hash"]),
            ),
            category_key=str(input_manifest["category_key"]),
            queue_class="production_batch",
            runtime_context={
                "run_mode": "incremental",
                **({key: frozen_context[key] for key in (
                    'incremental_execution_policy', 'incremental_binding_id',
                    'incremental_binding_lock_version')}
                   if frozen_context.get('incremental_execution_policy') is not None else {}),
                **({"incremental_route": snapshot["incremental_route"]} if "incremental_route" in snapshot else {}),
                "content_ingress_event_db_id": event.id,
                "content_ingress_event_id": event.event_id,
                "content_ingress_payload_hash": event.payload_hash,
                "content_record_id": input_manifest["content_record_id"],
                "material_package_id": package.id,
                "incremental_mechanism_snapshot_hash": snapshot["snapshot_hash"],
                "upstream_intake_contract_version_id": contract_id,
                "upstream_intake_contract_key": contract_key,
                "upstream_intake_payload_hash": input_hash,
                "batch_key": batch_key,
                "batch_trigger_reason": trigger_reason,
                "batch_size": policy["batch_size"],
                "max_wait_seconds": policy["max_wait_seconds"],
                "real_execution_confirmed": real_execution,
                "real_execution_authority": (
                    "frozen_incremental_autorun_policy"
                    if real_execution
                    else None
                ),
                "real_execution_budget": real_budget,
                "real_execution_node_limits": real_node_limits,
            },
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            owner="incremental-autorun",
            reason="上游内容接入后自动组批并进入冻结增量工作流",
            source_type="content_ingress_event",
            source_id=event.id,
            model_execution_mode=str(policy["model_execution_mode"]),
        ),
        actor=actor,
    )
    package.status = "incremental_sent"
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="content_ingress",
        action="incremental_run_dispatched",
        subject_type="production_run",
        subject_id=run.id,
        actor=actor,
        payload={
            "event_id": event.event_id,
            "content_record_id": input_manifest["content_record_id"],
            "material_package_id": package.id,
            "run_key": run.run_key,
            "batch_key": batch_key,
            "batch_trigger_reason": trigger_reason,
            "mechanism_snapshot_hash": snapshot["snapshot_hash"],
        },
        event_key=f"content-ingress:{event.event_id}:incremental-run",
    )
    from .connector_native_dispatch import link_connector_run
    link_connector_run(db, canonical=event, run=run)
    return run, True


def dispatch_content_event(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event: ContentIngressEvent,
    record: ContentRecord,
    duplicate: bool,
    actor: str,
) -> IncrementalDispatchResult:
    """Package and enqueue one ready content event exactly once."""

    package, package_created, routing_status = (
        route_content_event_to_incremental_package(
            db,
            event=event,
            record=record,
            duplicate=duplicate,
            actor=actor,
        )
    )
    if package is None:
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(
            package_id=None,
            package_created=False,
            batch_key=None,
            run_key=None,
            run_created=False,
            routing_status=routing_status,
        )
    if routing_status == "legacy_snapshot_review":
        # v91 and earlier packages never froze event-level input evidence.  They
        # remain queryable after upgrade, but replay must not guess a new input
        # manifest or accidentally dispatch paid work under changed facts.
        package.status = "incremental_legacy"
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(
            package_id=package.id,
            package_created=package_created,
            batch_key=None,
            run_key=None,
            run_created=False,
            routing_status=routing_status,
        )

    lane_key = str(package.incremental_lane_key or "")
    snapshot_hash = str(package.incremental_snapshot_hash or "")
    workflow_version_id = package.workflow_version_id
    existing = _existing_run(
        db,
        event=event,
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
    )
    if existing is not None:
        from .connector_native_dispatch import link_connector_run
        link_connector_run(db, canonical=event, run=existing)
        package.status = "incremental_sent"
        context = _run_context(existing)
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(
            package_id=package.id,
            package_created=package_created,
            batch_key=str(context.get("batch_key") or "") or None,
            run_key=existing.run_key,
            run_created=False,
            routing_status="dispatched",
        )

    if not lane_key or not snapshot_hash or workflow_version_id is None:
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_batch_lane_mismatch",
            "自动增量素材包缺少冻结 lane 身份",
        )
    frozen_category_key = str(package.category_key or "")
    expected_lane_key = incremental_lane_key(
        category_key=frozen_category_key,
        workflow_version_id=int(workflow_version_id),
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
    )
    route = _json_object(package.mechanism_snapshot_json, label="增量机制快照").get("incremental_route")
    if route is not None:
        expected_lane_key = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json([expected_lane_key, route["policy_id"], route["destination"]]).encode()).hexdigest()
    if (
        not frozen_category_key
        or package.incremental_event_id != event.id
        or event.content_record_id != record.id
        or lane_key != expected_lane_key
        or package.incremental_batch_size is None
        or package.incremental_due_at is None
    ):
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_batch_lane_mismatch",
            "自动增量素材包与冻结事件 lane 身份不一致",
        )
    current = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    active_backoff = _active_lane_backoff(
        db,
        lane_key=lane_key,
        category_key=frozen_category_key,
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
        current=current,
    )
    if active_backoff is not None:
        waiting_event_ids = _pending_lane_event_ids(
            db,
            lane_key=lane_key,
            category_key=frozen_category_key,
            workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
            snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
            limit=max(1, int(package.incremental_batch_size or 1)),
        )
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(
            package_id=package.id,
            package_created=package_created,
            batch_key=_batch_key(waiting_event_ids, snapshot_hash),
            run_key=None,
            run_created=False,
            routing_status="awaiting_batch",
        )

    snapshot = _mechanism_snapshot(package)
    policy = _autorun_policy(snapshot)
    frozen_input = snapshot["input_manifest"]
    if (
        str(frozen_input.get("category_key") or "") != frozen_category_key
        or snapshot.get("content_record_id") != event.content_record_id
        or package.incremental_batch_size != policy["batch_size"]
    ):
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_batch_lane_mismatch",
            "自动增量素材包与冻结机制 lane 身份不一致",
        )
    if not policy["enabled"]:
        package.status = "incremental_disabled"
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(
            package_id=package.id,
            package_created=package_created,
            batch_key=None,
            run_key=None,
            run_created=False,
            routing_status="awaiting_activation",
        )

    from .incremental_automation import automation_paused
    if automation_paused(db, category_key=frozen_category_key, workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id):
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(package_id=package.id, package_created=package_created,
            batch_key=None, run_key=None, run_created=False, routing_status="automation_paused")

    event_payload = _json_object(event.payload_json, label="内容接入事件载荷")
    priority = str(event_payload.get("priority") or event_payload.get("severity") or "")
    immediate = priority in policy["immediate_priorities"]
    pending = _pending_packages(
        db,
        lane_key=lane_key,
        category_key=frozen_category_key,
        workflow_version_id=snapshot["workflow_version_id"],
        snapshot_hash=snapshot["snapshot_hash"],
        limit=int(policy["batch_size"]),
    )
    if not any(item[0].id == package.id for item in pending):
        pending.append((package, event))
    # Auto-increment ids are the durable arrival order across SQLite/MySQL;
    # unlike driver-decoded datetimes they never mix aware and naive values.
    pending.sort(key=lambda item: item[0].id)

    trigger_reason: str | None = None
    selected: list[tuple[MaterialPackage, ContentIngressEvent]] = []
    if immediate:
        trigger_reason = "priority"
        selected = [(package, event)]
    elif len(pending) >= policy["batch_size"]:
        trigger_reason = "batch_size"
        selected = pending[: int(policy["batch_size"])]
    elif policy["max_wait_seconds"] == 0:
        # A zero wait explicitly means flush the currently available lane.
        trigger_reason = "max_wait"
        selected = pending[: int(policy["batch_size"])]
    elif pending:
        oldest = pending[0][0].created_at
        if oldest.tzinfo is None:
            oldest = oldest.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        age_seconds = max(
            0.0,
            (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - oldest).total_seconds(),
        )
        if age_seconds >= policy["max_wait_seconds"]:
            trigger_reason = "max_wait"
            selected = pending[: int(policy["batch_size"])]

    waiting_key = _batch_key(
        [item[1].event_id for item in pending],
        snapshot["snapshot_hash"],
    )
    if not selected or trigger_reason is None:
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(
            package_id=package.id,
            package_created=package_created,
            batch_key=waiting_key,
            run_key=None,
            run_created=False,
            routing_status="awaiting_batch",
        )

    batch_key = _batch_key(
        [item[1].event_id for item in selected],
        snapshot["snapshot_hash"],
    )
    current_run: ProductionRun | None = None
    current_created = False
    try:
        # Flush the ingress/package facts before opening the savepoint, then
        # keep paid run creation atomic per frozen batch.  A transient provider
        # or secret outage must not roll back the durable ingress event.
        with db.begin_nested():
            for selected_package, selected_event in selected:
                selected_snapshot = _mechanism_snapshot(selected_package)
                run, created = _create_run(
                    db,
                    package=selected_package,
                    event=selected_event,
                    snapshot=selected_snapshot,
                    policy=policy,
                    batch_key=batch_key,
                    trigger_reason=trigger_reason,
                    actor=actor,
                )
                if selected_package.id == package.id:
                    current_run = run
                    current_created = created
    except (IncrementalAutorunError, WorkflowRuntimeError) as exc:
        with db.begin_nested():
            if (
                _incremental_lane_error_code(exc)
                in PERMANENT_INCREMENTAL_LANE_ERROR_CODES
            ):
                _block_incremental_lane(
                    db,
                    lane_key=lane_key,
                    category_key=frozen_category_key,
                    workflow_version_id=int(workflow_version_id),
                    snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
                    actor=actor,
                    error=exc,
                )
                deferred_status = "incremental_blocked"
            else:
                _defer_incremental_lane(
                    db,
                    lane_key=lane_key,
                    category_key=frozen_category_key,
                    workflow_version_id=int(workflow_version_id),
                    snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
                    actor=actor,
                    current=current,
                    error=exc,
                )
                deferred_status = "awaiting_batch"
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(
            package_id=package.id,
            package_created=package_created,
            batch_key=batch_key,
            run_key=None,
            run_created=False,
            routing_status=deferred_status,
        )

    # A threshold flush can dispatch an older complete batch while leaving the
    # just-arrived event in the next batch.  Report that state precisely.
    if current_run is None:
        return IncrementalDispatchResult(
            package_id=package.id,
            package_created=package_created,
            batch_key=waiting_key,
            run_key=None,
            run_created=False,
            routing_status="awaiting_batch",
        )
    return IncrementalDispatchResult(
        package_id=package.id,
        package_created=package_created,
        batch_key=batch_key,
        run_key=current_run.run_key,
        run_created=current_created,
        routing_status="dispatched",
    )


def _block_incremental_lane(
    db: Session,
    *,
    lane_key: str,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    snapshot_hash: str,
    actor: str,
    error: IncrementalAutorunError | WorkflowRuntimeError,
) -> None:
    """Quarantine a deterministic bad lane without persisting error details."""

    result = db.execute(
        update(MaterialPackage)
        .where(
            MaterialPackage.source == "production_import",
            MaterialPackage.status == "incremental_pending",
            MaterialPackage.incremental_lane_key == lane_key,
            MaterialPackage.category_key == category_key,
            MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id == workflow_version_id,
            MaterialPackage.incremental_snapshot_hash == snapshot_hash,
        )
        .values(status="incremental_blocked")
    )
    error_code = _incremental_lane_error_code(error)
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="content_ingress",
        action="incremental_lane_blocked",
        subject_type="incremental_lane",
        subject_id=lane_key,
        actor=actor,
        payload={
            "lane_key": lane_key,
            "category_key": category_key,
            "workflow_version_id": workflow_version_id,
            "mechanism_snapshot_hash": snapshot_hash,
            "blocked_package_count": max(0, int(result.rowcount or 0)),
            "error_code": error_code,
        },
        event_key=f"incremental-lane:{lane_key}:blocked",
    )


def _incremental_lane_error_code(
    error: IncrementalAutorunError | WorkflowRuntimeError,
) -> str:
    return str(getattr(error, "code", type(error).__name__))[:80]


def _defer_incremental_lane(
    db: Session,
    *,
    lane_key: str,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    snapshot_hash: str,
    actor: str,
    current: datetime,
    error: IncrementalAutorunError | WorkflowRuntimeError,
) -> None:
    """Persist a bounded backoff so one bad lane cannot starve later lanes."""

    error_code = _incremental_lane_error_code(error)
    lane_filter = (
        MaterialPackage.source == "production_import",
        MaterialPackage.status == "incremental_pending",
        MaterialPackage.incremental_lane_key == lane_key,
        MaterialPackage.category_key == category_key,
        MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id == workflow_version_id,
        MaterialPackage.incremental_snapshot_hash == snapshot_hash,
    )
    previous_failures = db.scalar(
        select(
            func.max(
                func.coalesce(MaterialPackage.incremental_failure_count, 0)
            )
        ).where(*lane_filter)
    )
    failure_count = max(0, int(previous_failures or 0)) + 1
    exponent = min(failure_count - 1, 10)
    retry_seconds = min(
        MAX_INCREMENTAL_RETRY_SECONDS,
        INITIAL_INCREMENTAL_RETRY_SECONDS * (2**exponent),
    )
    retry_at = current + timedelta(seconds=retry_seconds)
    result = db.execute(
        update(MaterialPackage)
        .where(*lane_filter)
        .values(
            incremental_failure_count=failure_count,
            incremental_retry_at=retry_at,
        )
    )
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="content_ingress",
        action="incremental_lane_deferred",
        subject_type="incremental_lane",
        subject_id=lane_key,
        actor=actor,
        payload={
            "lane_key": lane_key,
            "category_key": category_key,
            "workflow_version_id": workflow_version_id,
            "mechanism_snapshot_hash": snapshot_hash,
            "error_code": error_code,
            "deferred_package_count": max(0, int(result.rowcount or 0)),
            "failure_count": failure_count,
            "retry_at": platform_isoformat(retry_at),
            "retry_policy": "bounded_exponential_backoff",
        },
        event_key=f"incremental-lane:{lane_key}:deferred:{error_code}",
    )


def _dispatch_due_incremental_lane(
    db: Session,
    *,
    lane: Any,
    seed: MaterialPackage,
    current: datetime,
    actor: str,
    remaining_run_capacity: int,
) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], bool]:
    """Dispatch one frozen lane inside the caller's database savepoint."""

    from .incremental_automation import automation_paused
    if automation_paused(db, category_key=str(lane.category_key), workflow_version_id=int(lane.workflow_version_id)):
        return (), False

    snapshot = _mechanism_snapshot(seed)
    policy = _autorun_policy(snapshot)
    if not policy["enabled"]:
        seed.status = "incremental_disabled"
        return (), False
    lane_key = str(lane.incremental_lane_key)
    category_key = str(lane.category_key)
    workflow_version_id = int(lane.workflow_version_id)
    snapshot_hash = str(lane.incremental_snapshot_hash)
    if (
        seed.incremental_lane_key != lane_key
        or seed.incremental_batch_size != policy["batch_size"]
        or snapshot["snapshot_hash"] != snapshot_hash
    ):
        raise IncrementalAutorunError(
            "incremental_batch_lane_mismatch",
            "自动增量 lane 索引与冻结策略不一致",
        )
    pending = _pending_packages(
        db,
        lane_key=lane_key,
        category_key=category_key,
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
        limit=int(policy["batch_size"]),
    )
    if not pending:
        return (), False
    pending.sort(key=lambda item: item[0].id)
    reason: str | None = None
    if len(pending) >= policy["batch_size"]:
        reason = "batch_size"
    else:
        oldest = pending[0][0].incremental_due_at
        if oldest is None:
            raise IncrementalAutorunError(
                "incremental_batch_lane_mismatch",
                "自动增量 lane 缺少到期时间",
            )
        if oldest.tzinfo is None:
            oldest = oldest.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        if policy["max_wait_seconds"] == 0 or current >= oldest:
            reason = "max_wait"
    if reason is None:
        return (), False
    # Never split a frozen logical batch merely to fill the tail of a worker
    # tick. It remains pending and becomes the first eligible lane next tick.
    if len(pending) > remaining_run_capacity:
        return (), True
    batch_key = _batch_key(
        [item[1].event_id for item in pending],
        snapshot_hash,
    )
    dispatched: list[str] = []
    for selected_package, selected_event in pending:
        run, created = _create_run(
            db,
            package=selected_package,
            event=selected_event,
